[CODE] (turn 1):
import math, numpy as np

def max_angle_spread(alpha):
    # endpoint at angle 0, other points at beta in (0, alpha]
    angles = []
    for beta in np.linspace(0, alpha, 10001):
        # vector from endpoint (1,0) to point at angle beta
        vx = math.cos(beta) - 1
        vy = math.sin(beta)
        ang = math.atan2(vy, vx)  # range -pi to pi
        angles.append(ang)
    # compute spread (max - min)
    spread = max(angles) - min(angles)
    return spread

for deg in [10,30,60,90,120,150,180]:
    rad = math.radians(deg)
    spread = max_angle_spread(rad)
    print(deg, math.degrees(spread))

import math, numpy as np

def max_angle_spread(alpha):
    # endpoint at angle 0, other points at beta in (0, alpha]
    angles = []
    for beta in np.linspace(0, alpha, 10001):
        # vector from endpoint (1,0) to point at angle beta
        vx = math.cos(beta) - 1
        vy = math.sin(beta)
        ang = math.atan2(vy, vx)  # range -pi to pi
        angles.append(ang)
    # compute spread (max - min)
    spread = max(angles) - min(angles)
    return spread

for deg in [10,30,60,90,120,150,180]:
    rad = math.radians(deg)
    spread = max_angle_spread(rad)
    print(deg, math.degrees(spread))

[CODE] (turn 2):
def find_alpha_for_spread(target_deg=100):
    lo, hi = 0.0, math.radians(180)
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = (lo+hi)/2
        spread = max_angle_spread(mid)
        if math.degrees(spread) > target_deg:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    return math.degrees(lo), math.degrees(hi)

find_alpha_for_spread()

[CODE] (turn 3):
import math, random, itertools, numpy as np

def max_visible(points, i, sector_angle_deg=100):
    # points: list of (x,y)
    xi, yi = points[i]
    angles = []
    for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
        if j == i: continue
        ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)  # -pi to pi
        angles.append(ang)
    # duplicate angles +2π for circular wrap
    angles = sorted(angles)
    m = len(angles)
    # convert to [0,2π)
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    angles.sort()
    # duplicate with +2π
    angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
    best = 0
    sector = math.radians(sector_angle_deg)
    # sliding window
    left = 0
    for right in range(len(angles2)):
        while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > sector + 1e-12:
            left += 1
        cnt = right - left + 1
        if cnt > best:
            best = cnt
    return best

def test(alpha_deg=20):
    n=100
    R=1.0
    al = math.radians(alpha_deg)
    # points on arc from 0 to al
    points = [(math.cos(t), math.sin(t)) for t in np.linspace(0, al, n)]
    # compute max visible per point
    maxes = [max_visible(points,i) for i in range(n)]
    return maxes

maxes = test(20)
min(maxes), max(maxes)

[CODE] (turn 4):
def spread_at_point(beta, alpha):
    # point at angle beta on unit circle, other points on arc [0,alpha]
    # compute angular spread of vectors to all other points
    angles = []
    for t in np.linspace(0, alpha, 1001):
        if abs(t-beta) < 1e-12: continue
        vx = math.cos(t) - math.cos(beta)
        vy = math.sin(t) - math.sin(beta)
        ang = math.atan2(vy, vx)
        if ang < 0: ang += 2*math.pi
        angles.append(ang)
    spread = max(angles) - min(angles)
    return spread

alpha = math.radians(20)
beta = alpha/2
math.degrees(spread_at_point(beta, alpha))

def spread_at_point(beta, alpha):

        vx = math.cos(t) - math.cos(beta)
        vy = math.sin(t) - math.sin(beta)
        ang = math.atan2(vy, vx)
        if ang < 0: ang += 2*math.pi
        angles.append(ang)

import math, numpy as np

def total_sum_on_circle(n=100, sector_deg=100):
    R=1.0
    points = [(math.cos(2*math.pi*i/n), math.sin(2*math.pi*i/n)) for i in range(n)]
    total=0
    for i in range(n):
        total+=max_visible(points,i,sector_deg)
    return total

[CODE] (turn 7):
import random, math, numpy as np

def random_circle_sum(n=100, trials=1000):
    best=0
    best_pts=None
    for _ in range(trials):
        angles = [random.random()*2*math.pi for _ in range(n)]
        points = [(math.cos(a), math.sin(a)) for a in angles]
        total = sum(max_visible(points,i) for i in range(n))
        if total>best:
            best=total
            best_pts=points
    return best

random_circle_sum(100, 200)

def test_uniform(n):
    angles = [math.radians(i*200/(n-1)) for i in range(n)]
    points = [(math.cos(a), math.sin(a)) for a in angles]
    counts = [max_visible(points,i) for i in range(n)]
    return counts

[CODE] (turn 17):
def direction_angle(theta_i, theta_j):
    xi, yi = math.cos(theta_i), math.sin(theta_i)
    xj, yj = math.cos(theta_j), math.sin(theta_j)
    ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
    if ang<0: ang+=2*math.pi
    return ang

theta_i = math.radians(100)
theta_j = math.radians(0)
print(math.degrees(direction_angle(theta_i, theta_j)))
print(math.degrees(((theta_i+theta_j)/2 + math.radians(90))% (2*math.pi)))

[CODE] (turn 18):
def sum_and_X(n=100):
    points = [(math.cos(2*math.pi*i/n), math.sin(2*math.pi*i/n)) for i in range(n)]
    total = sum(max_visible(points,i) for i in range(n))
    # compute X: number of unordered pairs with both directions counted
    # we can compute by checking for each unordered pair if both can see each other
    X=0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i+1,n):
            # check if i sees j and j sees i
            # compute direction angles
            # i sees j if clockwise distance from i to j <=200
            # compute angular positions
            theta_i = 2*math.pi*i/n
            theta_j = 2*math.pi*j/n
            # clockwise distance from i to j:
            d = (theta_j - theta_i) % (2*math.pi)
            deg = math.degrees(d)
            if deg <=200+1e-9 or deg >=160-1e-9: # not correct
                pass
    return total

sum_and_X()

[CODE] (turn 19):
def compute_X(points):
    n=len(points)
    # compute angles of points
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for x,y in points]
    # normalize to [0,2π)
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    X=0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i+1,n):
            # clockwise distance from i to j
            d_ij = (angles[j] - angles[i]) % (2*math.pi)
            deg_ij = math.degrees(d_ij)
            # i sees j if deg_ij <=200
            i_sees_j = deg_ij <= 200+1e-9
            # j sees i if (360 - deg_ij) <=200 => deg_ij >=160
            j_sees_i = deg_ij >= 160-1e-9
            if i_sees_j and j_sees_i:
                X+=1
    return X

# equally spaced
n=100
points = [(math.cos(2*math.pi*i/n), math.sin(2*math.pi*i/n)) for i in range(n)]
X = compute_X(points)
X

def compute_X(points):
    n=len(points)
    # compute angles of points
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for x,y in points]
    # normalize to [0,2π)
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    X=0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i+1,n):
            # clockwise distance from i to j
            d_ij = (angles[j] - angles[i]) % (2*math.pi)
            deg_ij = math.degrees(d_ij)
            # i sees j if deg_ij <=200
            i_sees_j = deg_ij <= 200+1e-9
            # j sees i if (360 - deg_ij) <=200 => deg_ij >=160
            j_sees_i = deg_ij >= 160-1e-9
            if i_sees_j and j_sees_i:
                X+=1
    return X

[CODE] (turn 21):
def both_see(points,i,j):
    # check if i sees j using max_visible orientation? Actually we need to check if there exists orientation for i that includes j and similarly for j.
    # But max_visible chooses optimal orientation; we need to see if j is within some 100° sector from i.
    # Equivalent to clockwise distance condition: i sees j iff clockwise distance <=200.
    # Let's compute using angles.
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for x,y in points]
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    theta_i = angles[i]
    theta_j = angles[j]
    d = (theta_j - theta_i) % (2*math.pi)
    deg = math.degrees(d)
    return deg <= 200+1e-9

def count_both(points):
    n=len(points)
    cnt=0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i+1,n):
            if both_see(points,i,j) and both_see(points,j,i):
                cnt+=1
    return cnt

[CODE] (turn 21):
def both_see(points,i,j):
    # check if i sees j using max_visible orientation? Actually we need to check if there exists orientation for i that includes j and similarly for j.
    # But max_visible chooses optimal orientation; we need to see if j is within some 100° sector from i.
    # Equivalent to clockwise distance condition: i sees j iff clockwise distance <=200.
    # Let's compute using angles.
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for x,y in points]
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    theta_i = angles[i]
    theta_j = angles[j]
    d = (theta_j - theta_i) % (2*math.pi)
    deg = math.degrees(d)
    return deg <= 200+1e-9

def count_both(points):
    n=len(points)
    cnt=0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i+1,n):
            if both_see(points,i,j) and both_see(points,j,i):
                cnt+=1
    return cnt

count_both(points)

def total_directed_edges(points):
    n=len(points)
    total=0
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for x,y in points]
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):
            if i==j: continue
            d = (angles[j] - angles[i]) % (2*math.pi)
            deg = math.degrees(d)
            if deg <= 200+1e-9:
                total+=1
    return total

total_directed_edges(points)

def cw_ccw_counts(points,i):
    n=len(points)
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for x,y in points]
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    theta_i = angles[i]
    cw=0
    ccw=0
    for j in range(n):
        if i==j: continue
        d = (angles[j] - theta_i) % (2*math.pi)
        deg = math.degrees(d)
        if deg <= 200+1e-9:
            cw+=1
        if (360-deg) <= 200+1e-9:
            ccw+=1
    return cw, ccw

cw, ccw = cw_ccw_counts(points,0)
cw, ccw

[CODE] (turn 27):
def best_interval(points,i,sector_deg=100):
    xi, yi = points[i]
    angs = []
    for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points):
        if j==i: continue
        ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
        if ang<0: ang+=2*math.pi
        angs.append(ang)
    angs.sort()
    m=len(angs)
    angs2 = angs + [a+2*math.pi for a in angs]
    sector = math.radians(sector_deg)
    best=0
    best_start=None
    left=0
    for right in range(len(angs2)):
        while angs2[right] - angs2[left] > sector + 1e-12:
            left+=1
        cnt = right-left+1
        if cnt>best:
            best=cnt
            best_start=angs2[left]
    return best, best_start

best, start = best_interval(points,0)
best, math.degrees(start)

[CODE] (turn 28):
def points_in_interval(points,i,start_deg,sector_deg=100):
    xi, yi = points[i]
    start = math.radians(start_deg)
    sector = math.radians(sector_deg)
    in_pts=[]
    for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points):
        if j==i: continue
        ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
        if ang<0: ang+=2*math.pi
        # normalize relative to start
        diff = (ang - start) % (2*math.pi)
        if diff <= sector+1e-12:
            in_pts.append(j)
    return in_pts

in_pts = points_in_interval(points,0,91.8)
len(in_pts)

def cw_distance(i,j,n=100):
    # angles i*2π/n, j*2π/n
    theta_i = 2*math.pi*i/n
    theta_j = 2*math.pi*j/n
    d = (theta_j - theta_i) % (2*math.pi)
    return math.degrees(d)

[CODE] (turn 2):
def max_in_wedge(points, apex_idx, angle_deg=100):
    # points: list of (x,y)
    ax, ay = points[apex_idx]
    angles = []
    for j, (bx,by) in enumerate(points):
        if j==apex_idx: continue
        ang = math.atan2(by - ay, bx - ax)  # range -pi to pi
        angles.append(ang)
    # sort angles
    angles.sort()
    # duplicate with +2pi for circular wrap
    n = len(angles)
    angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
    maxcnt = 0
    j = 0
    for i in range(n):
        while j < i+n and angles2[j] - angles2[i] <= math.radians(angle_deg)+1e-12:
            j+=1
        cnt = j - i
        if cnt>maxcnt:
            maxcnt=cnt
    return maxcnt

def total_sum(points, angle_deg=100):
    return sum(max_in_wedge(points,i,angle_deg) for i in range(len(points)))

def random_points_in_sector(n, radius=1.0, sector_angle_deg=10, center=(0,0)):
    # generate points uniformly within a sector of given angle at the circle center? Actually we want points near boundary maybe.
    # We'll generate points near a point on boundary: choose base point at (radius,0) and random offsets within small radius epsilon.
    base = (radius,0)
    eps = radius*0.1
    pts = []
    for _ in range(n):
        # random offset within circle of radius eps
        r = eps*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((base[0]+r*math.cos(theta), base[1]+r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

pts = random_points_in_sector(100)
print(total_sum(pts))

[CODE] (turn 4):
import math, random

def points_on_arc(n, R=1.0, theta=math.radians(30)):
    # place points uniformly on arc from angle -theta/2 to theta/2 on circle of radius R centered at (0,0)
    pts = []
    for i in range(n):
        ang = -theta/2 + theta * i/(n-1) if n>1 else 0
        pts.append((R*math.cos(ang), R*math.sin(ang)))
    return pts

def total_sum_for_arc(n, theta):
    pts = points_on_arc(n, R=1.0, theta=theta)
    return total_sum(pts)

for deg in [10,20,30,40,50,60,70,80,90]:
    print(deg, total_sum_for_arc(100, math.radians(deg)))

def points_on_arc(n, R=1.0, theta=math.radians(30)):
    # place points uniformly on arc from angle -theta/2 to theta/2 on circle of radius R centered at (0,0)
    pts = []
    for i in range(n):

[CODE] (turn 7):
import random, math, itertools, numpy as np, time, sys, math, random, itertools, numpy as np

def random_point_in_circle(R=1.0):
    # uniform distribution
    r = R*math.sqrt(random.random())
    theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
    return (r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))

def total_sum_random(n=100, trials=1000):
    best = 0
    best_pts = None
    for t in range(trials):
        pts = [random_point_in_circle() for _ in range(n)]
        s = total_sum(pts)
        if s > best:
            best = s
            best_pts = pts
    return best, best_pts

best, pts = total_sum_random(100, 200)
print("best sum", best)

import random, math, copy, time

def simulated_annealing(initial_pts, iterations=20000, temp_start=1.0, temp_end=0.001):
    n = len(initial_pts)
    pts = [list(p) for p in initial_pts]
    best_pts = copy.deepcopy(pts)
    best_score = total_sum(pts)
    score = best_score
    for it in range(iterations):
        # temperature schedule
        t = temp_start * ((temp_end/temp_start) ** (it/iterations))
        # pick a random point to move
        i = random.randrange(n)
        # propose new position: small random move within circle radius 1
        # ensure within unit circle
        angle = random.random()*2*math.pi
        radius = random.random()*0.1  # small step
        newx = pts[i][0] + radius*math.cos(angle)
        newy = pts[i][1] + radius*math.sin(angle)
        if newx**2 + newy**2 > 1.0:
            # reject move if outside circle
            continue
        old = pts[i][:]
        pts[i] = [newx, newy]
        new_score = total_sum(pts)
        delta = new_score - score
        if delta > 0 or random.random() < math.exp(delta / t):
            # accept
            score = new_score
            if new_score > best_score:
                best_score = new_score
                best_pts = copy.deepcopy(pts)
        else:
            # revert
            pts[i] = old
    return best_score, best_pts

# start with arc points
pts_arc = points_on_arc(100, theta=math.radians(150))  # 150 deg arc
print("initial sum", total_sum(pts_arc))
best_score, best_pts = simulated_annealing(pts_arc, iterations=20000)
print("best after SA", best_score)

import random, math, copy, time

def simulated_annealing(initial_pts, iterations=20000, temp_start=1.0, temp_end=0.001):
    n = len(initial_pts)
    pts = [list(p) for p in initial_pts]
    best_pts = copy.deepcopy(pts)
    best_score = total_sum(pts)
    score = best_score
    for it in range(iterations):
        # temperature schedule
        t = temp_start * ((temp_end/temp_start) ** (it/iterations))
        # pick a random point to move
        i = random.randrange(n)
        # propose new position: small random move within circle radius 1
        # ensure within unit circle
        angle = random.random()*2*math.pi
        radius = random.random()*0.1  # small step
        newx = pts[i][0] + radius*math.cos(angle)
        newy = pts[i][1] + radius*math.sin(angle)
        if newx**2 + newy**2 > 1.0:
            # reject move if outside circle
            continue

        pts[i] = [newx, newy]

        delta = new_score - score
        if delta > 0 or random.random() < math.exp(delta / t):
            # accept
            score = new_score
            if new_score > best_score:
                best_score = new_score
                best_pts = copy.deepcopy(pts)
        else:

            pts[i] = old

[CODE] (turn 13):
def total_sum_fast(pts):
    # compute a_i for each i using angular sorting O(n^2 log n)
    n = len(pts)
    total = 0
    for i in range(n):
        ax, ay = pts[i]
        angles = []
        for j in range(n):
            if j==i: continue
            bx, by = pts[j]
            ang = math.atan2(by - ay, bx - ax)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        n2 = len(angles)
        angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        maxcnt = 0
        j = 0
        for k in range(n2):
            while j < k+n2 and angles2[j] - angles2[k] <= math.radians(100)+1e-12:
                j+=1
            cnt = j - k
            if cnt > maxcnt:
                maxcnt = cnt
        total += maxcnt
    return total

def perturb_one(pts, step=0.05):
    n = len(pts)
    i = random.randrange(n)
    # random direction
    angle = random.random()*2*math.pi
    radius = random.random()*step
    newx = pts[i][0] + radius*math.cos(angle)
    newy = pts[i][1] + radius*math.sin(angle)
    if newx**2 + newy**2 > 1.0:
        return None
    new_pts = pts.copy()
    new_pts[i] = (newx,newy)
    return new_pts

def hill_climb(initial_pts, steps=5000, step=0.05):
    pts = initial_pts[:]
    best = total_sum_fast(pts)
    best_pts = pts[:]
    for _ in range(steps):
        new_pts = perturb_one(pts, step)
        if new_pts is None:
            continue
        new_score = total_sum_fast(new_pts)
        if new_score >= best:
            pts = new_pts
            best = new_score
            best_pts = new_pts
    return best, best_pts

pts_arc = points_on_arc(100, theta=math.radians(150))
print("initial sum", total_sum_fast(pts_arc))
best, best_pts = hill_climb(pts_arc, steps=200, step=0.02)
print("after hill climb", best)

import math, random

def points_on_arc(n, R=1.0, theta=math.radians(150)):
    pts = []
    for i in range(n):
        ang = -theta/2 + theta * i/(n-1) if n>1 else 0
        pts.append((R*math.cos(ang), R*math.sin(ang)))
    return pts

def total_sum_fast(pts):
    n = len(pts)
    total = 0
    for i in range(n):
        ax, ay = pts[i]
        angles = []
        for j in range(n):
            if j==i: continue
            bx, by = pts[j]
            ang = math.atan2(by - ay, bx - ax)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        n2 = len(angles)
        angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        maxcnt = 0
        j = 0
        for k in range(n2):
            while j < k+n2 and angles2[j] - angles2[k] <= math.radians(100)+1e-12:
                j+=1
            cnt = j - k
            if cnt > maxcnt:
                maxcnt = cnt
        total += maxcnt
    return total

def perturb_one(pts, step=0.05):
    n = len(pts)
    i = random.randrange(n)
    angle = random.random()*2*math.pi
    radius = random.random()*step
    newx = pts[i][0] + radius*math.cos(angle)
    newy = pts[i][1] + radius*math.sin(angle)
    if newx**2 + newy**2 > 1.0:
        return None
    new_pts = pts.copy()
    new_pts[i] = (newx,newy)
    return new_pts

def hill_climb(initial_pts, steps=500, step=0.05):
    pts = initial_pts[:]
    best = total_sum_fast(pts)
    best_pts = pts[:]
    for _ in range(steps):
        new_pts = perturb_one(pts, step)
        if new_pts is None:
            continue
        new_score = total_sum_fast(new_pts)
        if new_score >= best:
            pts = new_pts
            best = new_score
            best_pts = new_pts
    return best, best_pts

pts_arc = points_on_arc(100, theta=math.radians(150))
print("initial sum", total_sum_fast(pts_arc))
best, best_pts = hill_climb(pts_arc, steps=200, step=0.02)
print("after hill climb", best)

[CODE] (turn 15):
def random_points_inside_circle(n,R=1.0):
    pts=[]
    for _ in range(n):
        r = R*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

def try_random_hill(num_trials=20):
    best_global = 0
    best_config = None
    for _ in range(num_trials):
        pts = random_points_inside_circle(100)
        s0 = total_sum_fast(pts)
        best_local, _ = hill_climb(pts, steps=200, step=0.05)
        if best_local > best_global:
            best_global = best_local
            best_config = pts
    return best_global

print(try_random_hill(5))

[CODE] (turn 16):
def random_points_on_circle_arc(n, theta=math.radians(180)):
    # random angles within an arc of length theta centered at 0
    angles = [random.uniform(-theta/2, theta/2) for _ in range(n)]
    pts = [(math.cos(a), math.sin(a)) for a in angles]
    return pts

def avg_sum_random_arc(trials=100):
    s=0
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts = random_points_on_circle_arc(100, theta=math.radians(180))
        s+= total_sum_fast(pts)
    return s/trials

print(avg_sum_random_arc(20))

def a_i_via_central(pts):
    # pts on unit circle
    n=len(pts)
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for (x,y) in pts]
    # normalize to [0,2pi)
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    a_counts=[]
    for i in range(n):
        ai=0
        for j in range(n):
            if i==j: continue
            diff = (angles[j]-angles[i]) % (2*math.pi)
            # consider minimal angular distance along circle? Actually we need to check if chord direction lies within 100° wedge.
        a_counts.append(ai)
    return a_counts

[CODE] (turn 19):
def a_i_by_arc(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for (x,y) in pts]
    # normalize to [0,2π)
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    a_counts=[]
    for i in range(n):
        ai=0
        for j in range(n):
            if i==j: continue
            diff = (angles[j]-angles[i]) % (2*math.pi)
            # we consider the smaller of diff and 2π-diff? Not sure.
            # Let's count if diff <= 200° (in radians) or diff >= 2π-200°
            if diff <= math.radians(200) or diff >= 2*math.pi - math.radians(200):
                ai+=1
        a_counts.append(ai)
    return a_counts

def test_random_arc():
    pts = random_points_on_circle_arc(100, theta=math.radians(150))
    a1 = [max_in_wedge(pts,i) for i in range(100)]
    a2 = a_i_by_arc(pts)
    # compare
    return sum(a1), sum(a2), all(a1[i]==a2[i] for i in range(100))

print(test_random_arc())

def a_i_by_arc(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for (x,y) in pts]
    # normalize to [0,2π)
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    a_counts=[]
    for i in range(n):
        ai=0
        for j in range(n):
            if i==j: continue
            diff = (angles[j]-angles[i]) % (2*math.pi)
            # we consider the smaller of diff and 2π-diff? Not sure.
            # Let's count if diff <= 200° (in radians) or diff >= 2π-200°
            if diff <= math.radians(200) or diff >= 2*math.pi - math.radians(200):
                ai+=1
        a_counts.append(ai)
    return a_counts

    pts = random_points_on_circle_arc(100, theta=math.radians(150))
    a1 = [max_in_wedge(pts,i) for i in range(100)]
    a2 = a_i_by_arc(pts)

[CODE] (turn 20):
def max_in_wedge(points, apex_idx, angle_deg=100):
    ax, ay = points[apex_idx]
    angles = []
    for j, (bx,by) in enumerate(points):
        if j==apex_idx: continue
        ang = math.atan2(by - ay, bx - ax)
        angles.append(ang)
    angles.sort()
    n = len(angles)
    angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
    maxcnt = 0
    j = 0
    for i in range(n):
        while j < i+n and angles2[j] - angles2[i] <= math.radians(angle_deg)+1e-12:
            j+=1
        cnt = j - i
        if cnt > maxcnt:
            maxcnt = cnt
    return maxcnt

    return maxcnt

def random_points_on_circle_full(n):
    angles = [random.random()*2*math.pi for _ in range(n)]
    pts = [(math.cos(a), math.sin(a)) for a in angles]
    return pts

def avg_sum_full(trials=20):
    s=0
    maxs=0
    mins=1e9
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts = random_points_on_circle_full(100)
        val = total_sum_fast(pts)
        s+=val
        maxs=max(maxs,val)
        mins=min(mins,val)
    return s/trials, maxs, mins

def random_points_on_circle_full(n):
    angles = [random.random()*2*math.pi for _ in range(n)]
    pts = [(math.cos(a), math.sin(a)) for a in angles]
    return pts

def avg_sum_full(trials=20):
    s=0
    maxs=0
    mins=1e9
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts = random_points_on_circle_full(100)
        val = total_sum_fast(pts)
        s+=val
        maxs=max(maxs,val)
        mins=min(mins,val)
    return s/trials, maxs, mins

print(avg_sum_full(20))

[CODE] (turn 23):
def radial_projection(pts, R=1.0):
    proj=[]
    for x,y in pts:
        r = math.hypot(x,y)
        if r==0:
            # place at center? but cannot be at center because direction undefined; keep as is
            proj.append((0,0))
        else:
            factor = R/r
            proj.append((x*factor, y*factor))
    return proj

def compare_random():
    pts = random_points_inside_circle(100)
    s_inside = total_sum_fast(pts)
    proj = radial_projection(pts)
    s_proj = total_sum_fast(proj)
    return s_inside, s_proj

for _ in range(5):
    print(compare_random())

            proj.append((x*factor, y*factor))
    return proj

def a_i_by_arc_interval(pts, interval_deg=200):
    n=len(pts)
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for (x,y) in pts]
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    a_counts=[]
    L = math.radians(interval_deg)
    for i in range(n):
        # find max number of other points within interval length L
        # we can use sliding window
        sorted_angles = sorted(angles)
        # but we need interval that may start anywhere; we can duplicate list
        angs = sorted_angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in sorted_angles]
        maxcnt=0
        j=0
        for k in range(n):

                j+=1

        # This approach not correct for per i.

[CODE] (turn 26):
def max_in_200_arc(pts, i):
    # pts on unit circle
    n=len(pts)
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for (x,y) in pts]
    # normalize to [0,2π)
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    # we need to count other points j (j!=i) within some interval length 200°
    # We'll slide window over sorted angles (including i)
    sorted_angles = sorted(angles)
    # duplicate for wrap
    dup = sorted_angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in sorted_angles]
    maxcnt=0
    j=0
    for k in range(n):
        while j < k+n and dup[j] - dup[k] <= math.radians(200)+1e-12:
            j+=1
        cnt = j - k - 1  # exclude the point at k (which could be i or not)
        # But we need to ensure that i is within the interval considered? Actually interval can be any; we just count other points.
        # The interval we consider starts at dup[k]; we need to know if i's angle is within that interval.
        # However we are free to choose interval; we want max count of other points irrespective of i's inclusion.
        # So we just take max cnt over all intervals.
        if cnt > maxcnt:
            maxcnt = cnt
    return maxcnt

def compare_methods(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    a1 = [max_in_wedge(pts,i) for i in range(n)]
    a2 = [max_in_200_arc(pts,i) for i in range(n)]
    return a1, a2

pts = points_on_circle_angles([0,30,60,90,120])
a1,a2 = compare_methods(pts)
print(a1)
print(a2)

def max_in_200_arc(pts, i):
    # pts on unit circle
    n=len(pts)
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for (x,y) in pts]
    # normalize to [0,2π)
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    # we need to count other points j (j!=i) within some interval length 200°
    # We'll slide window over sorted angles (including i)
    sorted_angles = sorted(angles)
    # duplicate for wrap
    dup = sorted_angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in sorted_angles]
    maxcnt=0
    j=0
    for k in range(n):
        while j < k+n and dup[j] - dup[k] <= math.radians(200)+1e-12:
            j+=1
        cnt = j - k - 1  # exclude the point at k (which could be i or not)
        # But we need to ensure that i is within the interval considered? Actually interval can be any; we just count other points.
        # The interval we consider starts at dup[k]; we need to know if i's angle is within that interval.
        # However we are free to choose interval; we want max count of other points irrespective of i's inclusion.
        # So we just take max cnt over all intervals.
        if cnt > maxcnt:
            maxcnt = cnt
    return maxcnt

[CODE] (turn 27):
def a_i_via_200arc(pts, i):
    n=len(pts)
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for (x,y) in pts]
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    # we need to consider intervals of length 200° (in radians)
    L = math.radians(200)
    # duplicate list for wrap
    sorted_angles = sorted(angles)
    dup = sorted_angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in sorted_angles]
    maxcnt = 0
    j = 0
    for k in range(n):
        while j < k+n and dup[j] - dup[k] <= L + 1e-12:
            j+=1
        cnt = j - k  # number of points in interval including point at k
        if cnt > maxcnt:
            maxcnt = cnt
    # maxcnt includes possibly point i if its angle is within the interval; but we need max over intervals irrespective of containing i.
    # So a_i = maxcnt - 1 (exclude i itself)
    return maxcnt - 1

def compare_methods2(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    a1 = [max_in_wedge(pts,i) for i in range(n)]
    a2 = [a_i_via_200arc(pts,i) for i in range(n)]
    return a1, a2

pts = points_on_circle_angles([0,30,60,90,120])
a1,a2 = compare_methods2(pts)
print(a1)
print(a2)

[CODE] (turn 31):
def angles_from_i(pts,i):
    ax, ay = pts[i]
    angs=[]
    for j,(bx,by) in enumerate(pts):
        if j==i: continue
        ang = math.atan2(by - ay, bx - ax)
        angs.append(ang)
    return angs

angs = angles_from_i(pts,1)
print([math.degrees(a) for a in angs])

[CODE] (turn 34):
def sum_clockwise(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    angles = [math.atan2(y,x) for (x,y) in pts]
    angles = [(a if a>=0 else a+2*math.pi) for a in angles]
    # sort angles but keep original indices? We'll compute ordered pairs using angles.
    S=0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):
            if i==j: continue
            # clockwise distance from i to j:
            diff = (angles[j] - angles[i]) % (2*math.pi)
            if diff <= math.radians(200)+1e-12:
                S+=1
    return S

pts = points_on_arc(100, theta=math.radians(150))
print(total_sum_fast(pts))
print(sum_clockwise(pts))

def sum_clockwise(pts):

    # sort angles but keep original indices? We'll compute ordered pairs using angles.
    S=0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):
            if i==j: continue
            # clockwise distance from i to j:
            diff = (angles[j] - angles[i]) % (2*math.pi)
            if diff <= math.radians(200)+1e-12:
                S+=1
    return S

[CODE] (turn 1):
import random, math, itertools, sys, numpy as np

def compute_a(points):
    n = len(points)
    a = [0]*n
    for i, (xi, yi) in enumerate(points):
        # compute angles of vectors from i to others
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i==j: continue
            ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)  # range -pi to pi
            angles.append(ang)
        # sort angles
        angles.sort()
        # duplicate angles +2π for circular wrap
        m = len(angles)
        angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        maxcnt = 0
        # sliding window of width 100° = 100*pi/180 rad
        width = 100*math.pi/180
        left = 0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > width + 1e-12:
                left += 1
            cnt = right - left + 1
            if cnt > maxcnt:
                maxcnt = cnt
        a[i] = maxcnt
    return a

def test_arc(n=100, central_angle_deg=200):
    R = 1.0
    # place points on circle of radius R, central angle from -theta/2 to +theta/2
    theta = central_angle_deg*math.pi/180
    angles = np.linspace(-theta/2, theta/2, n)
    points = [(R*math.cos(a), R*math.sin(a)) for a in angles]
    a = compute_a(points)
    return a

a = test_arc()
max(a), min(a), sum(a)

import math
phi1 = -100*math.pi/180
phi2 = 100*math.pi/180
C = (math.cos(0), math.sin(0)) # (1,0)
A = (math.cos(phi1), math.sin(phi1))
B = (math.cos(phi2), math.sin(phi2))
def vec(P,Q):
    return (Q[0]-P[0], Q[1]-P[1])
v1 = vec(C,A)
v2 = vec(C,B)
def angle_between(v1,v2):
    dot = v1[0]*v2[0]+v1[1]*v2[1]
    norm1 = math.hypot(v1[0],v1[1])
    norm2 = math.hypot(v2[0],v2[1])
    return math.acos(dot/(norm1*norm2))
ang = angle_between(v1,v2)
ang*180/math.pi

    return (Q[0]-P[0], Q[1]-P[1])

v2 = vec(C,B)
def angle_between(v1,v2):
    dot = v1[0]*v2[0]+v1[1]*v2[1]
    norm1 = math.hypot(v1[0],v1[1])
    norm2 = math.hypot(v2[0],v2[1])
    return math.acos(dot/(norm1*norm2))
ang = angle_between(v1,v2)
ang*180/math.pi

def test_arc_angle(central_angle_deg):
    R=1.0
    n=100
    theta=central_angle_deg*math.pi/180
    angles = np.linspace(-theta/2, theta/2, n)

    a = compute_a(points)
    return max(a), min(a), sum(a)

[CODE] (turn 8):
def random_points(n=100):
    # uniform random within unit circle
    pts=[]
    for _ in range(n):
        r = math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

def avg_sum(trials=10):
    sums=[]
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts = random_points()
        a = compute_a(pts)
        sums.append(sum(a))
    return min(sums), max(sums), sum(sums)/len(sums)

avg_sum(5)

import random, math, itertools, numpy as np, sys, time, math, collections, itertools, math

def total_sum(points):
    a = compute_a(points)
    return sum(a)

def random_optimize(n=10, iterations=20000):
    # start with random points inside unit circle
    pts = random_points(n)
    best = total_sum(pts)
    best_pts = pts[:]
    for it in range(iterations):
        # perturb a random point slightly
        i = random.randrange(n)
        # new random point within circle
        r = math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
        new_pt = (r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))
        old_pt = pts[i]
        pts[i] = new_pt
        s = total_sum(pts)
        if s > best:
            best = s
            best_pts = pts[:]
        else:
            # revert with probability maybe
            pts[i] = old_pt
    return best, best_pts

best10, pts10 = random_optimize(10, 5000)
best10

def max_interval_width(points, i):
    # returns minimal angular width covering all other points (i.e., max count for width 100)
    # compute smallest interval that contains all points? Actually we want max count for width 100.
    # Let's compute the minimal angular interval covering all other points.
    xi, yi = points[i]
    angles = [math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi) for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points) if j!=i]
    angles.sort()
    # compute max gap
    gaps = [(angles[(k+1)%len(angles)] - angles[k]) % (2*math.pi) for k in range(len(angles))]
    max_gap = max(gaps)
    min_width = 2*math.pi - max_gap
    return min_width

for i in range(10):
    print(i, a_vals[i], max_interval_width(pts10,i)*180/math.pi)

def max_interval_width(points, i):
    # returns minimal angular width covering all other points (i.e., max count for width 100)
    # compute smallest interval that contains all points? Actually we want max count for width 100.
    # Let's compute the minimal angular interval covering all other points.
    xi, yi = points[i]
    angles = [math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi) for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points) if j!=i]
    angles.sort()
    # compute max gap
    gaps = [(angles[(k+1)%len(angles)] - angles[k]) % (2*math.pi) for k in range(len(angles))]
    max_gap = max(gaps)
    min_width = 2*math.pi - max_gap
    return min_width

def regular_polygon(n, radius=1.0):
    points = [(radius*math.cos(2*math.pi*k/n), radius*math.sin(2*math.pi*k/n)) for k in range(n)]
    return points

def sum_for_regular(n):
    pts = regular_polygon(n)
    return total_sum(pts)

def optimize_random(n, iterations=20000):
    # start with random points within unit circle
    pts = random_points(n)
    best = total_sum(pts)
    best_pts = pts[:]
    for it in range(iterations):
        i = random.randrange(n)
        old = pts[i]
        # small perturbation
        r = math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
        new_pt = (r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))
        pts[i] = new_pt
        s = total_sum(pts)
        if s > best:
            best = s
            best_pts = pts[:]
        else:
            # revert with some probability
            if random.random() < 0.5:
                pts[i] = old
    return best, best_pts

def search_n(n, trials=5):
    best_overall = 0
    for t in range(trials):
        best,_ = optimize_random(n, iterations=5000)
        if best > best_overall:
            best_overall = best
    return best_overall

for n in [6,8,10,12]:
    print("n", n, "best", search_n(n, trials=3))

[CODE] (turn 20):
def hill_climb(n, steps=20000):
    pts = random_points(n)
    best = total_sum(pts)
    best_pts = pts[:]
    for step in range(steps):
        # random move: pick a point and move slightly
        i = random.randrange(n)
        old = pts[i]
        # small perturbation within radius 0.1
        angle = random.random()*2*math.pi
        radius = random.random()*0.1
        new_pt = (old[0] + radius*math.cos(angle), old[1] + radius*math.sin(angle))
        # ensure inside unit circle
        if new_pt[0]**2 + new_pt[1]**2 > 1.0:
            continue
        pts[i] = new_pt
        s = total_sum(pts)
        if s > best:
            best = s
            best_pts = pts[:]
        else:
            # revert with probability
            if random.random() < 0.3:
                pts[i] = old
    return best, best_pts

def best_for_n(n, runs=10):
    best_overall = 0
    for r in range(runs):
        b,_ = hill_climb(n, steps=5000)
        if b > best_overall:
            best_overall = b
    return best_overall

for n in range(5,13):
    print(n, best_for_n(n, runs=5))

def hill_climb(n, steps=20000):
    pts = random_points(n)
    best = total_sum(pts)
    best_pts = pts[:]
    for step in range(steps):
        # random move: pick a point and move slightly
        i = random.randrange(n)
        old = pts[i]
        # small perturbation within radius 0.1
        angle = random.random()*2*math.pi
        radius = random.random()*0.1
        new_pt = (old[0] + radius*math.cos(angle), old[1] + radius*math.sin(angle))
        # ensure inside unit circle
        if new_pt[0]**2 + new_pt[1]**2 > 1.0:
            continue
        pts[i] = new_pt
        s = total_sum(pts)
        if s > best:
            best = s
            best_pts = pts[:]
        else:
            # revert with probability
            if random.random() < 0.3:
                pts[i] = old
    return best, best_pts

[CODE] (turn 26):
def random_search_best(n, trials=1000, steps=2000):
    best = 0
    best_pts = None
    for t in range(trials):
        pts = random_points(n)
        # simple hill climb
        for s in range(steps):
            i = random.randrange(n)
            old = pts[i]
            # random new point
            r = math.sqrt(random.random())
            theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
            new_pt = (r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))
            pts[i] = new_pt
            s_val = total_sum(pts)
            if s_val > best:
                best = s_val
                best_pts = pts[:]
            else:
                # revert with probability
                if random.random() < 0.3:
                    pts[i] = old
    return best, best_pts

best10, pts10b = random_search_best(10, trials=200, steps=500)
best10

[CODE] (turn 27):
def approximate_best(n, runs=10, steps=5000):
    best = 0
    for r in range(runs):
        pts = random_points(n)
        # hill climb
        for s in range(steps):
            i = random.randrange(n)
            old = pts[i]
            # small random move
            rdist = random.random()*0.2
            angle = random.random()*2*math.pi
            new_pt = (old[0] + rdist*math.cos(angle), old[1] + rdist*math.sin(angle))
            # keep inside unit circle
            if new_pt[0]**2 + new_pt[1]**2 > 1:
                continue
            pts[i] = new_pt
            s_val = total_sum(pts)
            if s_val > best:
                best = s_val
        # maybe random restart
    return best

for n in range(5,13):
    best = approximate_best(n, runs=5, steps=2000)
    print(n, best, n*(n-1)-best)

[CODE] (turn 27):
def approximate_best(n, runs=10, steps=5000):
    best = 0
    for r in range(runs):
        pts = random_points(n)
        # hill climb
        for s in range(steps):
            i = random.randrange(n)
            old = pts[i]
            # small random move
            rdist = random.random()*0.2
            angle = random.random()*2*math.pi
            new_pt = (old[0] + rdist*math.cos(angle), old[1] + rdist*math.sin(angle))
            # keep inside unit circle
            if new_pt[0]**2 + new_pt[1]**2 > 1:
                continue
            pts[i] = new_pt
            s_val = total_sum(pts)
            if s_val > best:
                best = s_val
        # maybe random restart
    return best

for n in range(5,13):
    best = approximate_best(n, runs=5, steps=2000)
    print(n, best, n*(n-1)-best)

[CODE] (turn 28):
import random, math, numpy as np, itertools, sys, time, math, collections

def random_angles(n):
    return sorted([random.random()*2*math.pi for _ in range(n)])

def points_on_circle(angles, radius=1.0):
    return [(radius*math.cos(a), radius*math.sin(a)) for a in angles]

def total_sum_angles(angles):
    pts = points_on_circle(angles)
    return total_sum(pts)

def hill_climb_angles(n, steps=5000):
    # start with random angles sorted
    angles = sorted([random.random()*2*math.pi for _ in range(n)])
    best = total_sum_angles(angles)
    best_angles = angles[:]
    for s in range(steps):
        # perturb one angle slightly
        i = random.randrange(n)
        old = angles[i]
        # small random shift
        delta = (random.random()*2-1)*0.1  # +-0.1 rad
        new = (old + delta) % (2*math.pi)
        # keep sorted order by swapping if needed
        angles[i] = new
        angles.sort()
        val = total_sum_angles(angles)
        if val > best:
            best = val
            best_angles = angles[:]
        else:
            # revert with probability
            if random.random() < 0.3:
                angles[i] = old
                angles.sort()
    return best, best_angles

def optimize_n(n, runs=5):
    best = 0
    for r in range(runs):
        val, ang = hill_climb_angles(n, steps=2000)
        if val > best:
            best = val
    return best

for n in [10,12,14,16]:
    print("n", n, "best", optimize_n(n, runs=3))

[CODE] (turn 29):
def total_sum_angle_width(points, width_deg):
    width = math.radians(width_deg)
    a = []
    for i, (xi, yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles = [math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi) for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points) if j!=i]
        angles.sort()
        angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        left = 0
        maxcnt = 0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > width + 1e-12:
                left += 1
            cnt = right - left + 1
            if cnt > maxcnt:
                maxcnt = cnt
        a.append(maxcnt)
    return sum(a)

def best_halfplane_sum(n, trials=200):
    best=0
    for t in range(trials):
        pts = random_points(n)
        s = total_sum_angle_width(pts, 180)
        if s>best:
            best=s
    return best

print(best_halfplane_sum(10, trials=200))

def generate_points_in_cone(n, cone_angle_deg=80, radius=1.0):
    apex = (0.0, 0.0)
    half = math.radians(cone_angle_deg/2)
    points = []
    for _ in range(n):
        r = random.random()*radius
        theta = random.uniform(-half, half)
        points.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))
    return points

def best_sum_cone(n, trials=200):
    best=0
    for t in range(trials):
        pts = generate_points_in_cone(n, cone_angle_deg=80)
        s = total_sum(pts)
        if s>best:
            best=s
    return best

print(best_sum_cone(10, trials=200))

[CODE] (turn 2):
def angular_spread(points, i):
    # points: list of (x,y)
    xi, yi = points[i]
    angles = []
    for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
        if j == i: continue
        ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)  # range -pi to pi
        angles.append(ang)
    # sort angles
    angles.sort()
    # duplicate with +2π for wrap-around
    angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
    # find minimal interval covering all angles: we need max gap between consecutive angles
    max_gap = max(angles2[k+len(angles)-1] - angles2[k] for k in range(len(angles)))
    # minimal covering interval length = 2π - max_gap
    min_interval = 2*math.pi - max_gap
    return min_interval

def max_out_degree(points):
    max_deg = 0
    for i in range(len(points)):
        spread = angular_spread(points, i)
        # if spread <= 100° in radians
        if spread <= math.radians(100):
            deg = len(points)-1
        else:
            # compute max number of points that can be covered by interval length 100°
            # sliding window
            xi, yi = points[i]
            angles = []
            for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
                if j == i: continue
                ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
                angles.append(ang)
            angles.sort()
            angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
            best = 0
            L = math.radians(100)
            j = 0
            for k in range(len(angles)):
                while j < k+len(angles) and angles2[j] - angles2[k] <= L + 1e-12:
                    j += 1
                best = max(best, j - k)
            deg = best
        max_deg = max(max_deg, deg)
    return max_deg

def total_sum(points):
    total = 0
    for i in range(len(points)):
        # compute max number of other points within interval length 100°
        xi, yi = points[i]
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if j == i: continue
            ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        best = 0
        L = math.radians(100)
        j = 0
        for k in range(len(angles)):
            while j < k+len(angles) and angles2[j] - angles2[k] <= L + 1e-12:
                j += 1
            best = max(best, j - k)
        total += best
    return total

def random_cluster(n, r):
    # cluster points within radius r around (0,0)
    pts = []
    for _ in range(n):
        # uniform in disk radius r
        rad = r*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((rad*math.cos(theta), rad*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

for r in [0.1, 0.01, 0.001, 0.0001]:
    pts = random_cluster(100, r)
    total = total_sum(pts)
    print(r, total)

[CODE] (turn 6):
def angular_spread_correct(points, i):
    xi, yi = points[i]
    angles = []
    for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
        if j == i: continue
        ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
        angles.append(ang)
    angles.sort()
    n = len(angles)
    # compute gaps
    max_gap = 0
    for k in range(n):
        next_ang = angles[(k+1)%n] + (2*math.pi if k == n-1 else 0)
        gap = next_ang - angles[k]
        if gap > max_gap:
            max_gap = gap
    min_interval = 2*math.pi - max_gap
    return min_interval

def total_sum_correct(points):
    total = 0
    L = math.radians(100)
    for i in range(len(points)):
        xi, yi = points[i]
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if j == i: continue
            ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        n = len(angles)
        # sliding window
        angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        best = 0
        j = 0
        for k in range(n):
            while j < k+n and angles2[j] - angles2[k] <= L + 1e-12:
                j += 1
            best = max(best, j - k)
        total += best
    return total

for r in [0.1, 0.01, 0.001, 0.0001]:
    pts = random_cluster(100, r)
    print(r, angular_spread_correct(pts, 0), total_sum_correct(pts))

def coverage_per_point(points):
    L = math.radians(100)
    cov = []
    for i in range(len(points)):
        xi, yi = points[i]
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if j == i: continue
            ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        n = len(angles)
        angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        best = 0
        j = 0
        for k in range(n):
            while j < k+n and angles2[j] - angles2[k] <= L + 1e-12:
                j += 1
            best = max(best, j - k)
        cov.append(best)
    return cov

def angular_spread_from_point(points, i):
    xi, yi = points[i]
    angles = []
    for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
        if j == i: continue
        angles.append(math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi))
    angles.sort()
    n = len(angles)
    max_gap = 0
    for k in range(n):
        next_ang = angles[(k+1)%n] + (2*math.pi if k == n-1 else 0)
        gap = next_ang - angles[k]
        max_gap = max(max_gap, gap)
    min_interval = 2*math.pi - max_gap
    return min_interval

# find point with min coverage
min_cov = min(cov)

[CODE] (turn 9):
def angular_spread_from_point(points, i):
    xi, yi = points[i]
    angles = []
    for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
        if j == i: continue
        angles.append(math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi))
    angles.sort()
    n = len(angles)
    max_gap = 0
    for k in range(n):
        next_ang = angles[(k+1)%n] + (2*math.pi if k == n-1 else 0)
        gap = next_ang - angles[k]
        max_gap = max(max_gap, gap)
    min_interval = 2*math.pi - max_gap
    return min_interval

# find point with min coverage
min_cov = min(cov)
idx = cov.index(min_cov)
print("min coverage", min_cov, "angular spread (deg)", math.degrees(angular_spread_from_point(pts, idx)))

[CODE] (turn 13):
def min_interval_len(angles):
    angles = sorted(angles)
    n = len(angles)
    max_gap = 0
    for k in range(n):
        next_ang = angles[(k+1)%n] + (2*math.pi if k == n-1 else 0)
        gap = next_ang - angles[k]
        if gap > max_gap:
            max_gap = gap
    return 2*math.pi - max_gap

def angular_spread_point(pts, i):
    xi, yi = pts[i]
    angles = [math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi) for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(pts) if j!=i]
    return min_interval_len(angles)

theta = math.radians(200)
pts = points_on_arc(100, 1.0, theta)
# pick a point near middle
phi = theta/2
# find index of point with angle phi (approx)
# we stored points as (cos a, sin a) for a uniform random angle; not sorted.
# Let's generate deterministic points at equally spaced angles for clarity.
def points_on_arc_eq(n, R, theta):
    angles = [i*theta/(n-1) for i in range(n)]
    return [(R*math.cos(a), R*math.sin(a)) for a in angles]

pts_eq = points_on_arc_eq(100, 1.0, theta)
mid_idx = 50  # middle point
print("spread mid point", math.degrees(angular_spread_point(pts_eq, mid_idx)))

def min_interval_len(angles):

    max_gap = 0

    return 2*math.pi - max_gap

import math
R=1.0
alpha = math.radians(200)
phi = alpha/2
# coordinates
P = (R*math.cos(phi), R*math.sin(phi))
A = (R*math.cos(0), R*math.sin(0))
B = (R*math.cos(alpha), R*math.sin(alpha))
def angle_between(v1, v2):
    # returns angle between vectors v1 and v2 (0 to pi)
    dot = v1[0]*v2[0] + v1[1]*v2[1]
    norm1 = math.hypot(v1[0], v1[1])
    norm2 = math.hypot(v2[0], v2[1])
    return math.acos(dot/(norm1*norm2))
vPA = (A[0]-P[0], A[1]-P[1])
vPB = (B[0]-P[0], B[1]-P[1])
ang = angle_between(vPA, vPB)
print(math.degrees(ang))

def points_on_circle_arc(n, R, alpha):
    # arc from -alpha/2 to alpha/2
    angles = [ -alpha/2 + i*alpha/(n-1) for i in range(n) ]
    return [(R*math.cos(a), R*math.sin(a)) for a in angles]

[CODE] (turn 18):
def random_points_in_circle(n, R=1.0):
    pts = []
    for _ in range(n):
        r = R*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

def total_sum_random_trials(trials=20):
    best = 0
    best_pts = None
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts = random_points_in_circle(100, 1.0)
        total = total_sum_correct(pts)
        if total > best:
            best = total
            best_pts = pts
    return best

print(total_sum_random_trials(30))

[CODE] (turn 23):
import random, math

def total_sum(points):
    return total_sum_correct(points)

def random_move(points, step=0.1):
    # move a random point slightly within circle radius 10
    n = len(points)
    i = random.randrange(n)
    x, y = points[i]
    # random displacement
    dx = (random.random()*2-1)*step
    dy = (random.random()*2-1)*step
    new_pt = (x+dx, y+dy)
    # ensure inside circle radius 10
    if new_pt[0]**2 + new_pt[1]**2 > 100: # radius 10
        return points
    new_points = points.copy()
    new_points[i] = new_pt
    return new_points

def hill_climb(initial, iterations=10000, step=0.5):
    best = initial
    best_val = total_sum(best)
    cur = best
    cur_val = best_val
    for it in range(iterations):
        cand = random_move(cur, step)
        cand_val = total_sum(cand)
        if cand_val > cur_val:
            cur = cand
            cur_val = cand_val
            if cand_val > best_val:
                best = cand
                best_val = cand_val
    return best_val, best

# start with line points
pts = points_on_line(100, length=10)
print("initial", total_sum(pts))
best_val, best_pts = hill_climb(pts, iterations=2000, step=0.2)
print("best after hill climb", best_val)

[CODE] (turn 31):
import math, random
def points_on_line(n, length=10):
    xs = [i*length/(n-1) for i in range(n)]
    return [(x, 0.0) for x in xs]

def total_sum_correct(points):
    L = math.radians(100)
    total = 0
    n = len(points)
    for i in range(n):
        xi, yi = points[i]
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if j == i: continue
            angles.append(math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi))
        angles.sort()
        angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        best = 0
        j = 0
        for k in range(len(angles)):
            while j < k+len(angles) and angles2[j] - angles2[k] <= L + 1e-12:
                j += 1
            best = max(best, j - k)
        total += best
    return total

def random_move(points, step=0.5):
    n = len(points)
    i = random.randrange(n)
    x, y = points[i]
    dx = (random.random()*2-1)*step
    dy = (random.random()*2-1)*step
    new_pt = (x+dx, y+dy)
    # keep within circle radius 10 (center at 0)
    if new_pt[0]**2 + new_pt[1]**2 > 100:
        return points
    new_points = points.copy()
    new_points[i] = new_pt
    return new_points

def hill_climb(initial, iterations=5000, step=0.5):
    best = initial
    best_val = total_sum_correct(best)
    cur = best
    cur_val = best_val
    for it in range(iterations):
        cand = random_move(cur, step)
        cand_val = total_sum_correct(cand)
        if cand_val > cur_val:
            cur = cand
            cur_val = cand_val
            if cand_val > best_val:
                best = cand
                best_val = cand_val
    return best_val, best

pts_line = points_on_line(100, length=10)
print("initial", total_sum_correct(pts_line))
best_val, best_pts = hill_climb(pts_line, iterations=20000, step=0.5)
print("best", best_val)

[CODE] (turn 31):
import math, random
def points_on_line(n, length=10):
    xs = [i*length/(n-1) for i in range(n)]
    return [(x, 0.0) for x in xs]

def total_sum_correct(points):
    L = math.radians(100)
    total = 0
    n = len(points)
    for i in range(n):
        xi, yi = points[i]
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if j == i: continue
            angles.append(math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi))
        angles.sort()
        angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        best = 0
        j = 0
        for k in range(len(angles)):
            while j < k+len(angles) and angles2[j] - angles2[k] <= L + 1e-12:
                j += 1
            best = max(best, j - k)
        total += best
    return total

def random_move(points, step=0.5):
    n = len(points)
    i = random.randrange(n)
    x, y = points[i]
    dx = (random.random()*2-1)*step
    dy = (random.random()*2-1)*step
    new_pt = (x+dx, y+dy)
    # keep within circle radius 10 (center at 0)
    if new_pt[0]**2 + new_pt[1]**2 > 100:
        return points
    new_points = points.copy()
    new_points[i] = new_pt
    return new_points

def hill_climb(initial, iterations=5000, step=0.5):
    best = initial
    best_val = total_sum_correct(best)
    cur = best
    cur_val = best_val
    for it in range(iterations):
        cand = random_move(cur, step)
        cand_val = total_sum_correct(cand)
        if cand_val > cur_val:
            cur = cand
            cur_val = cand_val
            if cand_val > best_val:
                best = cand
                best_val = cand_val
    return best_val, best

pts_line = points_on_line(100, length=10)
print("initial", total_sum_correct(pts_line))
best_val, best_pts = hill_climb(pts_line, iterations=20000, step=0.5)
print("best", best_val)

[CODE] (turn 1):
import random, math, itertools

def max_out_degree(point, others, angle_width=100):
    # compute angles from point to each other point in degrees [0,360)
    angles = []
    x0,y0 = point
    for (x,y) in others:
        dx,dy = x-x0, y-y0
        ang = math.degrees(math.atan2(dy,dx)) % 360
        angles.append(ang)
    # sort angles
    angles.sort()
    # duplicate list with +360 for wrap-around
    angles2 = angles + [a+360 for a in angles]
    maxcnt = 0
    j=0
    n=len(angles)
    for i in range(n):
        # move j while angle difference <= angle_width
        while j < i+n and angles2[j] - angles2[i] <= angle_width + 1e-9:
            j+=1
        cnt = j - i
        if cnt > maxcnt:
            maxcnt = cnt
    return maxcnt

def total_sum(points):
    n=len(points)
    total=0
    for i,p in enumerate(points):
        others = points[:i]+points[i+1:]
        total+=max_out_degree(p, others)
    return total

def test_random(n, trials=1000):
    maxfound=0
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts=[(random.random(), random.random()) for _ in range(n)]
        total=total_sum(pts)
        if total>maxfound:
            maxfound=total
    return maxfound

for n in range(2,8):
    max_rand = test_random(n, trials=200)
    print(n, max_rand, n*(n-1)//2)

import random, math, itertools

def max_out_degree(point, others, angle_width=100):
    angles = []
    x0,y0 = point
    for (x,y) in others:
        dx,dy = x-x0, y-y0
        ang = math.degrees(math.atan2(dy,dx)) % 360
        angles.append(ang)
    angles.sort()
    # duplicate for wrap
    angles2 = angles + [a+360 for a in angles]
    maxcnt = 0
    j=0
    n=len(angles)
    for i in range(n):
        while j < i+n and angles2[j] - angles2[i] <= angle_width + 1e-9:
            j+=1
        cnt = j - i
        if cnt > maxcnt:
            maxcnt = cnt
    return maxcnt

def total_sum(points, angle_width=100):
    total=0
    n=len(points)
    for i,p in enumerate(points):
        others = points[:i]+points[i+1:]
        total+=max_out_degree(p, others, angle_width)
    return total

def random_point_in_circle(radius=1):
    # uniform distribution
    r = radius*math.sqrt(random.random())
    theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
    return (r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))

def search_max(n, trials=2000):
    best=0
    best_pts=None
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts=[random_point_in_circle() for __ in range(n)]
        s=total_sum(pts)
        if s>best:
            best=s
            best_pts=pts
    return best, best_pts

for n in range(2,9):
    best,_=search_max(n, trials=2000)
    print(n, best, n*(n-1)//2)

[CODE] (turn 13):
import random, math

def random_point_in_circle(radius=1):
    r = radius*math.sqrt(random.random())
    theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
    return (r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))

def max_out_degree(point, others, angle_width=100):
    angles = []
    x0,y0 = point
    for (x,y) in others:
        dx,dy = x-x0, y-y0
        ang = math.degrees(math.atan2(dy,dx)) % 360
        angles.append(ang)
    angles.sort()
    angles2 = angles + [a+360 for a in angles]
    maxcnt = 0
    j=0
    n=len(angles)
    for i in range(n):
        while j < i+n and angles2[j] - angles2[i] <= angle_width + 1e-9:
            j+=1
        cnt = j - i
        if cnt > maxcnt:
            maxcnt = cnt
    return maxcnt

def total_sum(points, angle_width=100):
    total=0
    n=len(points)
    for i,p in enumerate(points):
        others = points[:i]+points[i+1:]
        total+=max_out_degree(p, others, angle_width)
    return total

def approx_max_sum_fast(n, trials=5000):
    best=0
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts=[random_point_in_circle() for __ in range(n)]
        s=total_sum(pts)
        if s>best:
            best=s
    return best

for n in range(5,13):
    best=approx_max_sum_fast(n, trials=20000)
    print(n, best, n*(n-1)-best)

def construct_points(n, group_size=4, spacing=10.0, intra=0.1):
    points=[]
    groups = n // group_size
    remainder = n % group_size
    for g in range(groups):
        base = g * spacing
        for i in range(group_size):
            points.append((base + i*intra, 0.0))
    # remainder group
    base = groups * spacing
    for i in range(remainder):
        points.append((base + i*intra, 0.0))
    return points

pts100 = construct_points(100, group_size=4, spacing=10.0, intra=0.1)
s100 = total_sum(pts100)
s100

def points_on_arc(num, start_angle=0, end_angle=80, radius=1):
    pts=[]
    for i in range(num):
        theta = math.radians(start_angle + (end_angle-start_angle)*(i/(num-1)))
        pts.append((radius*math.cos(theta), radius*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

def sum_a(points):
    return total_sum(points)

for n in range(5,11):
    pts = points_on_arc(n, start_angle=0, end_angle=80, radius=1)
    print(n, sum_a(pts))

[CODE] (turn 23):
def construct_V(n, split=None, angle=100, spacing=1.0):
    if split is None:
        split = n//2
    pts=[]
    # ray 1 along positive x-axis
    for i in range(split):
        pts.append((i*spacing, 0.0))
    # ray 2 at angle degrees from x-axis
    theta = math.radians(angle)
    for i in range(n - split):
        pts.append((i*spacing*math.cos(theta), i*spacing*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

ptsV = construct_V(100, split=50, angle=100, spacing=1.0)
sV = total_sum(ptsV)
sV

def construct_V_angle(n, split, angle_deg, spacing=1.0):
    pts=[]
    for i in range(split):
        pts.append((i*spacing, 0.0))
    theta = math.radians(angle_deg)
    for i in range(n-split):
        pts.append((i*spacing*math.cos(theta), i*spacing*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

def max_sum_V(n):
    best=0
    best_params=None
    for angle in range(10,101,5):
        for split in range(0,n+1):
            pts = construct_V_angle(n, split, angle)
            s = total_sum(pts)
            if s>best:
                best=s
                best_params=(angle, split)
    return best, best_params

bestV, params = max_sum_V(30)
bestV, params

[CODE] (turn 27):
def angle_from_A_to_B(d, d_prime, theta_deg=100):
    theta = math.radians(theta_deg)
    dx = d_prime*math.cos(theta) - d
    dy = d_prime*math.sin(theta)
    ang = math.degrees(math.atan2(dy, dx)) % 360
    return ang

def max_angle_seen(d, max_dist, theta_deg=100):
    # compute max angle among points on other ray at distances 0..max_dist
    angles = [angle_from_A_to_B(d, dp, theta_deg) for dp in range(max_dist+1)]
    return min(angles), max(angles)

for d in [10,20,30]:
    mn,mx = max_angle_seen(d, 30)
    print(d, mn, mx)

[CODE] (turn 2):
def sum_a(points):
    n = len(points)
    total = 0
    for i, (xi, yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i == j: continue
            ang = math.degrees(math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)) % 360
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        # duplicate angles +360 for circular wrap
        angles2 = angles + [a+360 for a in angles]
        maxcnt = 0
        m = len(angles)
        left = 0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > 100 + 1e-9:  # >100
                left += 1
            cnt = right - left + 1
            if cnt > maxcnt:
                maxcnt = cnt
        total += maxcnt
    return total

def random_points(n, radius=1.0):
    pts = []
    for _ in range(n):
        # uniform inside circle radius
        r = radius * math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

for _ in range(5):
    pts = random_points(100, radius=10)
    print(sum_a(pts))

def cluster_points(n, center=(0,0), radius=0.01):
    cx, cy = center
    pts = []
    for _ in range(n):
        r = radius * math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((cx + r*math.cos(theta), cy + r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

for rad in [0.1, 0.01, 0.001]:
    pts = cluster_points(100, radius=rad)
    print(rad, sum_a(pts))

def arc_points(n, radius=10, arc_angle_deg=30):
    # arc from -arc_angle/2 to +arc_angle/2
    pts = []
    for _ in range(n):
        theta = math.radians(-arc_angle_deg/2 + random.random()*arc_angle_deg)
        r = radius * (0.9 + 0.1*random.random())  # near radius
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

for arc in [30, 60, 90, 120]:
    pts = arc_points(100, radius=10, arc_angle_deg=arc)
    print(arc, sum_a(pts))

def strip_points(n, length=10, width=0.1):
    pts = []
    for _ in range(n):
        x = random.random()*length
        y = (random.random()-0.5)*width
        pts.append((x, y))
    return pts

for w in [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.5,1]:
    pts = strip_points(100, length=10, width=w)
    print(w, sum_a(pts))

[CODE] (turn 8):
def strip_points(n, length=10, width=0.1):
    pts = []
    for _ in range(n):
        x = random.random()*length
        y = (random.random()-0.5)*width
        pts.append((x, y))
    return pts

for w in [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.5,1]:
    pts = strip_points(100, length=10, width=w)
    print(w, sum_a(pts))

[CODE] (turn 10):
def a_i_list(points):
    n = len(points)
    a = []
    for i, (xi, yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i == j: continue
            ang = math.degrees(math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)) % 360
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2 = angles + [a+360 for a in angles]
        maxcnt = 0
        left = 0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > 100 + 1e-9:
                left += 1
            cnt = right - left + 1
            maxcnt = max(maxcnt, cnt)
        a.append(maxcnt)
    return a

a_vals = a_i_list(pts)
print(a_vals[:10])
print(a_vals[-10:])
print(min(a_vals), max(a_vals), sum(a_vals))

            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2 = angles + [a+360 for a in angles]
        maxcnt = 0

        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > 100 + 1e-9:

        a.append(maxcnt)

[CODE] (turn 12):
def perturb(points, sigma=0.5):
    # random small move for each point
    new = []
    for (x,y) in points:
        nx = x + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        ny = y + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        new.append((nx, ny))
    return new

def hill_climb(initial, iterations=1000, sigma=0.5):
    best = initial
    best_score = sum_a(best)
    for it in range(iterations):
        cand = perturb(best, sigma)
        score = sum_a(cand)
        if score > best_score:
            best = cand
            best_score = score
            # print progress
            # print(it, best_score)
    return best, best_score

# start with line arrangement
init = line_eq_points(100, length=100)
print("initial", sum_a(init))
best_pts, best_score = hill_climb(init, iterations=200, sigma=5.0)
print("best", best_score)

def perturb(points, sigma=0.5):

    new = []
    for (x,y) in points:
        nx = x + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        ny = y + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        new.append((nx, ny))
    return new

def hill_climb(initial, iterations=1000, sigma=0.5):
    best = initial
    best_score = sum_a(best)
    for it in range(iterations):
        cand = perturb(best, sigma)
        score = sum_a(cand)
        if score > best_score:
            best = cand
            best_score = score
            # print progress
            # print(it, best_score)
    return best, best_score

[CODE] (turn 13):
def random_init():
    # random points inside circle radius 100
    pts = []
    for _ in range(100):
        r = 100 * math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

init = random_init()
print("initial random", sum_a(init))
best_pts, best_score = hill_climb(init, iterations=500, sigma=10.0)
print("best after hill", best_score)

        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

[CODE] (turn 14):
def anneal(initial, steps=5000, start_sigma=20.0, end_sigma=0.1):
    current = initial
    cur_score = sum_a(current)
    best = current
    best_score = cur_score
    for step in range(steps):
        # linear schedule for sigma
        sigma = start_sigma + (end_sigma - start_sigma) * step / steps
        # propose move: perturb one random point slightly
        i = random.randrange(len(current))
        x,y = current[i]
        nx = x + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        ny = y + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        new = current.copy()
        new[i] = (nx, ny)
        new_score = sum_a(new)
        # accept if better or with probability exp((new - cur)/temp)
        # temperature schedule maybe decreasing
        temp = max(1.0, (steps - step)/steps)  # simple
        if new_score > cur_score or random.random() < math.exp((new_score - cur_score)/temp):
            current = new
            cur_score = new_score
            if new_score > best_score:
                best = new
                best_score = new_score
    return best, best_score

init = random_init()
best_pts, best_score = anneal(init, steps=2000, start_sigma=30.0, end_sigma=0.1)
print(best_score)

[CODE] (turn 14):
def anneal(initial, steps=5000, start_sigma=20.0, end_sigma=0.1):
    current = initial
    cur_score = sum_a(current)
    best = current
    best_score = cur_score
    for step in range(steps):
        # linear schedule for sigma
        sigma = start_sigma + (end_sigma - start_sigma) * step / steps
        # propose move: perturb one random point slightly

        x,y = current[i]
        nx = x + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        ny = y + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        new = current.copy()
        new[i] = (nx, ny)
        new_score = sum_a(new)
        # accept if better or with probability exp((new - cur)/temp)
        # temperature schedule maybe decreasing
        temp = max(1.0, (steps - step)/steps)  # simple
        if new_score > cur_score or random.random() < math.exp((new_score - cur_score)/temp):
            current = new
            cur_score = new_score
            if new_score > best_score:
                best = new
                best_score = new_score
    return best, best_score

init = random_init()

[CODE] (turn 15):
def run_trials(trials=10):
    best_overall = 0
    best_pts = None
    for t in range(trials):
        init = random_init()
        pts, score = anneal(init, steps=3000, start_sigma=30.0, end_sigma=0.1)
        print("Trial", t, "score", score)
        if score > best_overall:
            best_overall = score
            best_pts = pts
    return best_overall, best_pts

best_score, best_pts = run_trials(5)
print("Overall best", best_score)

[CODE] (turn 16):
def anneal_fast(initial, steps=5000, start_sigma=20.0, end_sigma=0.1):
    current = initial[:]
    cur_score = sum_a(current)
    best = current[:]
    best_score = cur_score
    for step in range(steps):
        sigma = start_sigma + (end_sigma - start_sigma) * step / steps
        i = random.randrange(len(current))
        x,y = current[i]
        nx = x + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        ny = y + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        new = current[:]
        new[i] = (nx, ny)
        new_score = sum_a(new)
        if new_score > cur_score or random.random() < 0.001:  # small acceptance for downhill
            current = new
            cur_score = new_score
            if new_score > best_score:
                best = new[:]
                best_score = new_score
    return best, best_score

init = random_init()
best, score = anneal_fast(init, steps=2000, start_sigma=30, end_sigma=0.1)
print(score)

def sum_a(points):
    n = len(points)
    total = 0
    for i, (xi, yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i == j: continue
            ang = math.degrees(math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)) % 360
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2 = angles + [a+360 for a in angles]
        maxcnt = 0
        left = 0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > 100 + 1e-9:
                left += 1
            cnt = right - left + 1
            if cnt > maxcnt:
                maxcnt = cnt
        total += maxcnt
    return total

import random, math

def sum_a(points):
    n = len(points)
    total = 0
    for i, (xi, yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i == j: continue
            ang = math.degrees(math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)) % 360
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2 = angles + [a+360 for a in angles]
        maxcnt = 0
        left = 0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > 100 + 1e-9:
                left += 1
            cnt = right - left + 1
            if cnt > maxcnt:
                maxcnt = cnt
        total += maxcnt
    return total

def random_init():
    pts = []
    for _ in range(100):
        r = 100 * math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

def anneal_fast2(initial, steps=5000, start_sigma=30.0, end_sigma=0.1):
    current = initial[:]
    cur_score = sum_a(current)
    best = current[:]
    best_score = cur_score
    for step in range(steps):
        sigma = start_sigma + (end_sigma - start_sigma) * step / steps
        i = random.randrange(len(current))
        x,y = current[i]
        nx = x + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        ny = y + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        new = current[:]
        new[i] = (nx, ny)
        new_score = sum_a(new)
        if new_score > cur_score:
            current = new
            cur_score = new_score
            if new_score > best_score:
                best = new[:]
                best_score = new_score
    return best, best_score

best, score = anneal_fast2(random_init(), steps=4000, start_sigma=30, end_sigma=0.1)
print(score)

import random, math

def sum_a(points):
    n = len(points)
    total = 0
    for i, (xi, yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i == j: continue
            ang = math.degrees(math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)) % 360
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2 = angles + [a+360 for a in angles]
        maxcnt = 0
        left = 0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > 100 + 1e-9:
                left += 1
            cnt = right - left + 1
            if cnt > maxcnt:
                maxcnt = cnt
        total += maxcnt
    return total

def random_init():
    pts = []
    for _ in range(100):
        r = 100 * math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

def anneal_fast2(initial, steps=5000, start_sigma=30.0, end_sigma=0.1):
    current = initial[:]
    cur_score = sum_a(current)
    best = current[:]
    best_score = cur_score
    for step in range(steps):
        sigma = start_sigma + (end_sigma - start_sigma) * step / steps
        i = random.randrange(len(current))
        x,y = current[i]
        nx = x + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        ny = y + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        new = current[:]
        new[i] = (nx, ny)
        new_score = sum_a(new)
        if new_score > cur_score:
            current = new
            cur_score = new_score
            if new_score > best_score:
                best = new[:]
                best_score = new_score
    return best, best_score

best, score = anneal_fast2(random_init(), steps=4000, start_sigma=30, end_sigma=0.1)
print(score)
