import random, math

def sum_a(points):
    total = 0
    n = len(points)
    for i, (xi, yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i == j: continue
            ang = math.degrees(math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)) % 360
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2 = angles + [a+360 for a in angles]
        maxcnt = 0
        left = 0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > 100 + 1e-9:
                left += 1
            cnt = right - left + 1
            if cnt > maxcnt:
                maxcnt = cnt
        total += maxcnt
    return total

def a_i_list(points):
    n = len(points)
    a = []
    for i, (xi, yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i == j: continue
            ang = math.degrees(math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)) % 360
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2 = angles + [a+360 for a in angles]
        maxcnt = 0
        left = 0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > 100 + 1e-9:
                left += 1
            cnt = right - left + 1
            if cnt > maxcnt:
                maxcnt = cnt
        a.append(maxcnt)
    return a

def hill_climb_one_step(points, steps=1000, sigma=1.0):
    best = points[:]
    best_score = sum_a(best)
    for _ in range(steps):
        i = random.randrange(len(best))
        x,y = best[i]
        nx = x + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        ny = y + random.gauss(0, sigma)
        new = best[:]
        new[i] = (nx, ny)
        new_score = sum_a(new)
        if new_score > best_score:
            best = new
            best_score = new_score
    return best, best_score

[CODE] (turn 2):
import random, math, itertools, sys, time, numpy as np

def max_sum_random(n, trials=20000):
    best = 0
    best_pts = None
    for t in range(trials):
        # random points uniformly in unit circle
        pts = []
        for _ in range(n):
            r = math.sqrt(random.random())
            theta = random.random()*2*math.pi
            pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
        # compute sum
        total = 0
        for i in range(n):
            angles = []
            xi, yi = pts[i]
            for j in range(n):
                if i==j: continue
                xj, yj = pts[j]
                ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)  # -pi to pi
                angles.append(ang)
            # sort angles
            angles.sort()
            # duplicate with +2pi for circular wrap
            angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
            # sliding window width = 100 deg = 100*pi/180 rad
            width = 100*math.pi/180
            maxcnt = 0
            left = 0
            for right in range(len(angles2)):
                while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > width + 1e-12:
                    left += 1
                cnt = right - left + 1
                if cnt > maxcnt:
                    maxcnt = cnt
            total += maxcnt
        if total > best:
            best = total
            best_pts = pts
    return best, best_pts

for n in range(3,9):
    best,_ = max_sum_random(n, trials=2000)
    print(n, best)

def max_sum_random(n, trials=20000):

    best_pts = None
    for t in range(trials):

        pts = []

                angles.append(ang)
            # sort angles
            angles.sort()

            angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]

def total_sum(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    total=0
    width=100*math.pi/180
    for i in range(n):
        angles=[]
        xi,yi=pts[i]
        for j in range(n):
            if i==j: continue
            xj,yj=pts[j]
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi,xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        maxcnt=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left]>width+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            if cnt>maxcnt:
                maxcnt=cnt
        total+=maxcnt
    return total

def random_optimize(n, iterations=20000, step=0.05):
    # start with random points
    pts=[(random.random()*2-1, random.random()*2-1) for _ in range(n)]
    # ensure inside unit circle; if not, reflect
    # We'll just keep them inside unit circle by rejection
    def inside(p):
        return p[0]**2 + p[1]**2 <= 1.0
    pts=[p for p in pts if inside(p)]
    while len(pts)<n:
        p=(random.random()*2-1, random.random()*2-1)
        if inside(p):
            pts.append(p)
    best=total_sum(pts)
    best_pts=pts[:]
    for it in range(iterations):
        # random perturb one point
        i=random.randrange(n)
        x,y=pts[i]
        # propose new point within small radius
        angle=random.random()*2*math.pi
        r=step*random.random()
        nx=x+r*math.cos(angle)
        ny=y+r*math.sin(angle)
        if nx*nx+ny*ny>1:
            continue
        old=pts[i]
        pts[i]=(nx,ny)
        s=total_sum(pts)
        if s>best:
            best=s
            best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            # revert with probability? simple hill climbing
            pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts

for n in [9,10,11,12]:
    best,_=random_optimize(n, iterations=5000, step=0.2)
    print(n, best)

            if cnt>maxcnt:
                maxcnt=cnt
        total+=maxcnt
    return total

[CODE] (turn 7):
def collinear_sum_formula(n):
    if n%2==0:
        m=n//2
        return 3*m*m - m
    else:
        m=(n-1)//2
        # compute directly
        return sum(max(p, n-1-p) for p in range(n))
for n in range(3,15):
    print(n, collinear_sum(n))

        m=n//2
        return 3*m*m - m
    else:
        m=(n-1)//2
        # compute directly
        return sum(max(p, n-1-p) for p in range(n))

[CODE] (turn 8):
def points_on_arc(n, theta_deg):
    theta = math.radians(theta_deg)
    # choose equally spaced angles within [-theta/2, theta/2]
    angles = [ -theta/2 + i*theta/(n-1) for i in range(n) ]
    pts = [(math.cos(a), math.sin(a)) for a in angles]
    return pts

def total_for_arc(n, theta_deg):
    pts = points_on_arc(n, theta_deg)
    return total_sum(pts)

for theta in [80,90,95,99,100,110]:
    print(theta, total_for_arc(12, theta))

def random_cluster(n, radius=0.01):
    pts = [(random.uniform(-radius, radius), random.uniform(-radius, radius)) for _ in range(n)]
    return pts

def best_cluster(n, trials=2000, radius=0.01):
    best=0
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts = random_cluster(n, radius)
        s = total_sum(pts)
        if s>best:
            best=s
    return best

print(best_cluster(12, trials=2000, radius=0.01))

def best_random_cluster_n(n, trials=5000, radius=0.02):
    best=0
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts = [(random.uniform(-radius, radius), random.uniform(-radius, radius)) for _ in range(n)]
        s = total_sum(pts)
        if s>best:
            best=s
    return best

for n in [25,30,40]:
    best=best_random_cluster_n(n, trials=2000, radius=0.02)
    print(n, best, "collinear", collinear_sum(n), "diff", best-collinear_sum(n))

[CODE] (turn 16):
import random, math, time, sys, itertools, numpy as np, copy, math, random, sys, time

def total_sum_pts(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    total=0
    width=100*math.pi/180
    for i in range(n):
        xi,yi=pts[i]
        angles=[]
        for j in range(n):
            if i==j: continue
            xj,yj=pts[j]
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        maxcnt=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left]>width+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            if cnt>maxcnt:
                maxcnt=cnt
        total+=maxcnt
    return total

def simulated_anneal(n, iterations=200000, radius=1.0):
    # start with collinear points along x-axis inside circle radius 1
    pts=[((i-(n-1)/2)*0.01, 0.0) for i in range(n)]  # small spacing
    # scale to fit inside unit circle
    # ensure within radius
    # compute current total
    cur=total_sum_pts(pts)
    best=cur
    best_pts=pts[:]
    T0=1.0
    for it in range(iterations):
        T = T0 * (1 - it/iterations)
        # propose move: pick random point, move slightly
        i=random.randrange(n)
        x,y=pts[i]
        # random displacement
        dx=(random.random()*2-1)*0.02
        dy=(random.random()*2-1)*0.02
        nx,ny=x+dx, y+dy
        if nx*nx+ny*ny>radius*radius:
            continue
        old=pts[i]
        pts[i]=(nx,ny)
        new=total_sum_pts(pts)
        delta=new-cur
        if delta>0 or random.random()<math.exp(delta/T if T>0 else 0):
            cur=new
            if cur>best:
                best=cur
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts

best, pts=simulated_anneal(30, iterations=50000)
best

def simulated_anneal_fixed_radius(n, radius=0.5, iterations=200000):
    # start with random points inside radius
    pts=[(random.uniform(-radius, radius), random.uniform(-radius, radius)) for _ in range(n)]
    # ensure inside unit circle (radius <=1)
    cur=total_sum_pts(pts)
    best=cur
    best_pts=pts[:]
    T0=1.0
    for it in range(iterations):
        T = T0 * (1 - it/iterations)
        i=random.randrange(n)
        x,y=pts[i]
        dx=(random.random()*2-1)*0.02*radius
        dy=(random.random()*2-1)*0.02*radius
        nx,ny=x+dx,y+dy
        if nx*nx+ny*ny>radius*radius:
            continue
        old=pts[i]
        pts[i]=(nx,ny)
        new=total_sum_pts(pts)
        delta=new-cur
        if delta>0 or random.random()<math.exp(delta/T if T>0 else 0):
            cur=new
            if cur>best:
                best=cur
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts

import random, math, time

def total_sum_pts(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    width=100*math.pi/180
    total=0
    for i in range(n):
        xi,yi=pts[i]
        angles=[]
        for j in range(n):
            if i==j: continue
            xj,yj=pts[j]
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        maxcnt=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left]>width+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            if cnt>maxcnt:
                maxcnt=cnt
        total+=maxcnt
    return total

def simulated_anneal_fixed_radius(n, radius=0.5, iterations=5000):
    pts=[(random.uniform(-radius, radius), random.uniform(-radius, radius)) for _ in range(n)]
    # ensure inside circle
    pts=[p for p in pts if p[0]**2+p[1]**2<=radius**2]
    while len(pts)<n:
        x=random.uniform(-radius, radius)
        y=random.uniform(-radius, radius)
        if x*x+y*y<=radius*radius:
            pts.append((x,y))
    cur=total_sum_pts(pts)
    best=cur
    best_pts=pts[:]
    T0=1.0
    for it in range(iterations):
        T = T0 * (1 - it/iterations)
        i=random.randrange(n)
        x,y=pts[i]
        dx=(random.random()*2-1)*0.05*radius
        dy=(random.random()*2-1)*0.05*radius
        nx,ny=x+dx,y+dy
        if nx*nx+ny*ny>radius*radius:
            continue
        old=pts[i]
        pts[i]=(nx,ny)
        new=total_sum_pts(pts)
        delta=new-cur
        if delta>0 or random.random()<math.exp(delta/T if T>0 else 0):
            cur=new
            if cur>best:
                best=cur
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts

best100, pts100 = simulated_anneal_fixed_radius(100, radius=0.5, iterations=2000)

def collinear_points(n, spacing=0.01):
    return [((i-(n-1)/2)*spacing, 0.0) for i in range(n)]

def collinear_sum(n):
    return sum(max(p, n-1-p) for p in range(n))

def improve_from_initial(pts, iterations=5000, step=0.02):
    n=len(pts)
    cur=total_sum_pts(pts)
    best=cur
    best_pts=pts[:]
    T0=1.0
    for it in range(iterations):
        T = T0 * (1 - it/iterations)
        i=random.randrange(n)
        x,y=pts[i]
        dx=(random.random()*2-1)*step
        dy=(random.random()*2-1)*step
        nx,ny=x+dx,y+dy
        if nx*nx+ny*ny>1: # unit circle radius 1
            continue
        old=pts[i]
        pts[i]=(nx,ny)
        new=total_sum_pts(pts)
        delta=new-cur
        if delta>0 or random.random()<math.exp(delta/T if T>0 else 0):
            cur=new
            if cur>best:
                best=cur
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts

        dy=(random.random()*2-1)*step

        pts[i]=(nx,ny)
        new=total_sum_pts(pts)
        delta=new-cur
        if delta>0 or random.random()<math.exp(delta/T if T>0 else 0):
            cur=new
            if cur>best:
                best=cur
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts

for n in range(28,41):
    pts=collinear_points(n, spacing=0.01)
    best,_=improve_from_initial(pts, iterations=5000, step=0.02)
    print(n, best, collinear_sum(n), best-collinear_sum(n))

def parabola_points(n, scale=0.5):
    xs = np.linspace(-scale, scale, n)
    pts = [(x, x*x) for x in xs]
    # shift to fit inside unit circle (center at 0)
    # ensure within circle radius 1
    # maybe scale down
    max_r = max(math.hypot(x,y) for x,y in pts)
    factor = 0.9 / max_r
    pts = [(x*factor, y*factor) for x,y in pts]
    return pts

pts = parabola_points(100)
total_sum_pts(pts)

def improve_random_initial(n, iterations=2000, step=0.02):
    # start with random points inside unit circle
    pts=[]
    while len(pts)<n:
x=random.uniform(-1,1)
y=random.uniform(-1,1)
if x*x+y*y<=1:
pts.append((x,y))
    cur=total_sum_pts(pts)
    best=cur
    best_pts=pts[:]
    T0=1.0
    for it in range(iterations):
T=T0*(1-it/iterations)
i=random.randrange(n)
x,y=pts[i]
dx=(random.random()*2-1)*step
dy=(random.random()*2-1)*step
nx,ny=x+dx,y+dy
if nx*nx+ny*ny>1:
continue
old=pts[i]
pts[i]=(nx,ny)
new=total_sum_pts(pts)
delta=new-cur
if delta>0 or random.random()<math.exp(delta/T if T>0 else 0):
cur=new
if cur>best:
    best=cur
    best_pts=pts[:]
else:
pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts

[CODE] (turn 34):
def arc_points(n, radius=1.0, theta_deg=120):
    theta=math.radians(theta_deg)
    # place points equally spaced along arc from -theta/2 to theta/2
    angles=[-theta/2 + i*theta/(n-1) for i in range(n)]
    pts=[(radius*math.cos(a), radius*math.sin(a)) for a in angles]
    return pts

def max_a_i(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    width=100*math.pi/180
    a=[]
    for i in range(n):
        xi,yi=pts[i]
        angs=[]
        for j in range(n):
            if i==j: continue
            xj,yj=pts[j]
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angs.append(ang)
        angs.sort()
        angs2=angs+[a+2*math.pi for a in angs]
        maxcnt=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angs2)):
            while angs2[right]-angs2[left]>width+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            if cnt>maxcnt:
                maxcnt=cnt
        a.append(maxcnt)
    return a

for theta in [60,80,100,120,150]:
    pts=arc_points(100, radius=1.0, theta_deg=theta)
    a=max_a_i(pts)
    total=sum(a)
    print(theta, total)

[CODE] (turn 35):
def v_shape_points(n, angle_deg=80, length=1.0):
    # split points roughly equally on two arms
    n1 = n//2
    n2 = n - n1
    angle = math.radians(angle_deg)
    # arm1 along direction -angle/2, arm2 along +angle/2
    pts=[]
    for k in range(n1):
        t = (k+1)/(n1+1)  # avoid vertex point itself
        x = t*length*math.cos(-angle/2)
        y = t*length*math.sin(-angle/2)
        pts.append((x,y))
    for k in range(n2):
        t = (k+1)/(n2+1)
        x = t*length*math.cos(angle/2)
        y = t*length*math.sin(angle/2)
        pts.append((x,y))
    # ensure within unit circle
    max_r = max(math.hypot(x,y) for x,y in pts)
    if max_r>1:
        factor = 0.9/max_r
        pts = [(x*factor, y*factor) for x,y in pts]
    return pts

pts = v_shape_points(100, angle_deg=80, length=1.0)
total_sum_pts(pts)

        pts.append((x,y))

def max_total_vshape(n, angles_deg, length=1.0):
    best=0
    best_angle=None
    for ang in angles_deg:
        pts=v_shape_points(n, angle_deg=ang, length=length)
        total=total_sum_pts(pts)
        if total>best:
            best=total
            best_angle=ang
    return best, best_angle

best,total_angle = max_total_vshape(100, angles)
best, total_angle

def vshape_points_ratio(n, angle_deg=58.8, length=0.5, ratio=0.5):
    n1 = int(round(n*ratio))
    n2 = n - n1
    angle = math.radians(angle_deg)
    pts=[]
    for k in range(n1):
        t = (k+1)/(n1+1) if n1>0 else 0
        x = t*length*math.cos(-angle/2)
        y = t*length*math.sin(-angle/2)
        pts.append((x,y))
    for k in range(n2):
        t = (k+1)/(n2+1) if n2>0 else 0
        x = t*length*math.cos(angle/2)
        y = t*length*math.sin(angle/2)
        pts.append((x,y))
    # scale to fit inside unit circle
    max_r = max(math.hypot(x,y) for x,y in pts)
    if max_r>1:
        factor = 0.9/max_r
        pts = [(x*factor, y*factor) for x,y in pts]
    return pts

def best_ratio(n, angle_deg, length):
    best=0
    best_r=0
    for r in [i/100 for i in range(10,91,5)]:  # 0.1 to 0.9 step 0.05
        pts=vshape_points_ratio(n, angle_deg, length, r)
        total=total_sum_pts(pts)
        if total>best:
            best=total
            best_r=r
    return best, best_r

best, best_r = best_ratio(100, 58.8, 0.5)
best, best_r

import random, math, itertools, numpy as np

def total_sum(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    L=100*math.pi/180
    total=0
    for i,(xi,yi) in enumerate(pts):
        angles=[]
        for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(pts):
            if i==j: continue
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi,xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        # sort
        angles.sort()
        # duplicate with +2π
        m=len(angles)
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        best=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left] > L+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            if cnt>best: best=cnt
        total+=best
    return total

def line_sum(n):
    pts=[(i,0) for i in range(n)]
    return total_sum(pts)

print(line_sum(6))

def random_sum(n, trials=20000):
    best=0
    best_pts=None
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts=[(random.random(), random.random()) for _ in range(n)]
        s=total_sum(pts)
        if s>best:
            best=s
            best_pts=pts
    return best, best_pts

def per_point_counts(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    L=100*math.pi/180
    counts=[]
    for i,(xi,yi) in enumerate(pts):
        angles=[]
        for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(pts):
            if i==j: continue
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi,xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        best=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left] > L+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            best=max(best,cnt)
        counts.append(best)
    return counts

per_point_counts(pts)

def arc_points(n, angle_range_deg=80, radius=1):
    angles = [math.radians(random.uniform(0, angle_range_deg)) for _ in range(n)]
    pts = [(radius*math.cos(a), radius*math.sin(a)) for a in angles]
    return pts

def test_arc(n, trials=2000):
    best=0
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts=arc_points(n, angle_range_deg=80)
        s=total_sum(pts)
        if s>best:
            best=s
    return best

[CODE] (turn 12):
def max_spread_from_point(pt, others):
    xi, yi = pt
    angles = [math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi) for (xj,yj) in others]
    # compute max difference modulo 2π
    # sort and compute max gap complement
    angles = sorted(angles)
    # compute max angular distance between any two points
    maxdiff = max((angles[(i+1)%len(angles)] - angles[i]) % (2*math.pi) for i in range(len(angles)))
    # Actually we need minimal covering interval length = 2π - maxgap
    maxgap = max((angles[(i+1)%len(angles)] - angles[i]) % (2*math.pi) for i in range(len(angles)))
    min_interval = 2*math.pi - maxgap
    return min_interval

def test_sector(n, angle_range_deg=80, trials=1000):
    best=0
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts = [(random.random()*math.cos(math.radians(random.uniform(0, angle_range_deg))),
                random.random()*math.sin(math.radians(random.uniform(0, angle_range_deg))) for _ in range(n)]

def max_spread_from_point(pt, others):
    xi, yi = pt
    angles = [math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi) for (xj,yj) in others]

    # Actually we need minimal covering interval length = 2π - maxgap
    maxgap = max((angles[(i+1)%len(angles)] - angles[i]) % (2*math.pi) for i in range(len(angles)))

[CODE] (turn 13):
def random_points_in_sector(n, angle_range_deg=80, radius=1):
    pts=[]
    for _ in range(n):
        r = random.random()*radius
        theta = math.radians(random.uniform(0, angle_range_deg))
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

def max_interval_len(pt, others):
    angles = [math.atan2(y-pt[1], x-pt[0]) for (x,y) in others]
    angles.sort()
    # duplicate
    m=len(angles)
    angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
    L=0
    left=0
    for right in range(len(angles2)):
        while angles2[right]-angles2[left] > math.pi*2: # not needed
            left+=1
        # we want minimal covering interval length = max difference between min and max
    # Actually compute minimal interval length covering all angles:
    # maxgap = max difference between consecutive angles (including wrap)
    maxgap = max((angles[(i+1)%m] - angles[i]) % (2*math.pi) for i in range(m))
    min_interval = 2*math.pi - maxgap
    return min_interval

def max_spread_in_sector(n, angle_range_deg=80, trials=200):
    max_spread=0
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts = random_points_in_sector(n, angle_range_deg)
        spreads = [max_interval_len(p, [q for q in pts if q!=p]) for p in pts]
        max_spread = max(max_spread, max(spreads))
    return max_spread

print(max_spread_in_sector(10, 80, 200))

def random_points_in_sector(n, angle_range_deg=80, radius=1):
    pts=[]
    for _ in range(n):
        r = random.random()*radius
        theta = math.radians(random.uniform(0, angle_range_deg))
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

def max_interval_len(pt, others):
    angles = [math.atan2(y-pt[1], x-pt[0]) for (x,y) in others]
    angles.sort()
    # duplicate
    m=len(angles)
    angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
    L=0
    left=0
    for right in range(len(angles2)):
        while angles2[right]-angles2[left] > math.pi*2: # not needed
            left+=1
        # we want minimal covering interval length = max difference between min and max
    # Actually compute minimal interval length covering all angles:
    # maxgap = max difference between consecutive angles (including wrap)
    maxgap = max((angles[(i+1)%m] - angles[i]) % (2*math.pi) for i in range(m))
    min_interval = 2*math.pi - maxgap
    return min_interval

def max_spread_in_sector(n, angle_range_deg=80, trials=200):
    max_spread=0
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts = random_points_in_sector(n, angle_range_deg)
        spreads = [max_interval_len(p, [q for q in pts if q!=p]) for p in pts]
        max_spread = max(max_spread, max(spreads))
    return max_spread

[CODE] (turn 14):
import random, math, copy, time, itertools, sys, numpy as np

def total_sum_fast(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    L=100*math.pi/180
    total=0
    for i in range(n):
        xi, yi = pts[i]
        angles = []
        for j in range(n):
            if i==j: continue
            xj, yj = pts[j]
            ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        m=len(angles)
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        best=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left] > L+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            if cnt>best: best=cnt
        total+=best
    return total

def simulated_anneal(n, steps=50000, temp0=1.0, cooling=0.999):
    # initialize points on line from (0,0) to (1,0) with small random y perturbations
    pts=[(i/(n-1), random.uniform(-0.01,0.01)) for i in range(n)]
    best_pts=copy.deepcopy(pts)
    best_val=total_sum_fast(pts)
    val=best_val
    temp=temp0
    for step in range(steps):
        # random move: pick a point and perturb
        i=random.randrange(n)
        x,y=pts[i]
        # propose new position within small radius
        nx = x + random.uniform(-0.02,0.02)
        ny = y + random.uniform(-0.02,0.02)
        # keep within unit square
        nx = max(0.0, min(1.0, nx))
        ny = max(0.0, min(1.0, ny))
        old=pts[i]
        pts[i]=(nx,ny)
        new_val=total_sum_fast(pts)
        delta=new_val - val
        if delta>0 or random.random() < math.exp(delta/temp):
            val=new_val
            if val>best_val:
                best_val=val
                best_pts=copy.deepcopy(pts)
        else:
            pts[i]=old
        temp*=cooling
    return best_val, best_pts

best20, pts20 = simulated_anneal(20, steps=20000)
best20

import math, random, copy, numpy as np

def total_sum_fast(pts):
    n=len(pts)
    L=100*math.pi/180
    total=0
    for i in range(n):
        xi, yi = pts[i]
        # compute angles to others using numpy
        xs = np.array([p[0] for p in pts])
        ys = np.array([p[1] for p in pts])
        dx = xs - xi
        dy = ys - yi
        angles = np.arctan2(dy, dx)
        # remove self angle
        angles = np.delete(angles, i)
        angles = np.sort(angles)
        m = len(angles)
        angles2 = np.concatenate((angles, angles + 2*math.pi))
        best = 0
        left = 0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > L + 1e-12:
                left += 1
            cnt = right - left + 1
            if cnt > best:
                best = cnt
        total += best
    return int(total)

def simulated_anneal(n, steps=5000, temp0=1.0, cooling=0.995):
    pts=[(i/(n-1), random.uniform(-0.01,0.01)) for i in range(n)]
    best_pts=copy.deepcopy(pts)
    best_val=total_sum_fast(pts)
    val=best_val
    temp=temp0
    for step in range(steps):
        i=random.randrange(n)
        x,y=pts[i]
        nx = x + random.uniform(-0.02,0.02)
        ny = y + random.uniform(-0.02,0.02)
        nx = max(0.0, min(1.0, nx))
        ny = max(0.0, min(1.0, ny))
        old=pts[i]
        pts[i]=(nx,ny)
        new_val=total_sum_fast(pts)
        delta = new_val - val
        if delta > 0 or random.random() < math.exp(delta/temp):
            val = new_val
            if val > best_val:
                best_val = val
                best_pts = copy.deepcopy(pts)
        else:
            pts[i]=old
        temp *= cooling
    return best_val, best_pts

def line_sum(n):
    return sum(max(i-1, n-i) for i in range(1,n+1))

print("line sum 100:", line_sum(100))

def total_sum_fast(pts):

    L=100*math.pi/180
    total=0
    for i in range(n):

        total += best
    return int(total)

[CODE] (turn 1):
import math, random, itertools, numpy as np

def max_angle_spread(theta, n=100):
    # points on unit circle at angles equally spaced in [0, theta]
    angles = np.linspace(0, theta, n)
    max_spread = 0
    for i, phi0 in enumerate(angles):
        # compute direction angles from point i to others
        # point coordinates
        x0, y0 = math.cos(phi0), math.sin(phi0)
        dirs = []
        for j, phi in enumerate(angles):
            if i==j: continue
            x, y = math.cos(phi), math.sin(phi)
            dx, dy = x - x0, y - y0
            ang = math.atan2(dy, dx) % (2*math.pi)
            dirs.append(ang)
        # compute minimal angular interval covering all dirs (circular)
        dirs.sort()
        # compute max gap
        gaps = [(dirs[(k+1)%len(dirs)] - dirs[k]) % (2*math.pi) for k in range(len(dirs))]
        max_gap = max(gaps)
        spread = 2*math.pi - max_gap
        max_spread = max(max_spread, spread)
    return max_spread

for deg in [10,30,60,90,100,120]:
    rad = math.radians(deg)
    spread = max_angle_spread(rad, n=30)
    print(deg, math.degrees(spread))

[CODE] (turn 1):
import math, random, itertools, numpy as np

def max_angle_spread(theta, n=100):
    # points on unit circle at angles equally spaced in [0, theta]
    angles = np.linspace(0, theta, n)
    max_spread = 0
    for i, phi0 in enumerate(angles):
        # compute direction angles from point i to others
        # point coordinates
        x0, y0 = math.cos(phi0), math.sin(phi0)
        dirs = []
        for j, phi in enumerate(angles):
            if i==j: continue
            x, y = math.cos(phi), math.sin(phi)
            dx, dy = x - x0, y - y0
            ang = math.atan2(dy, dx) % (2*math.pi)
            dirs.append(ang)
        # compute minimal angular interval covering all dirs (circular)
        dirs.sort()
        # compute max gap
        gaps = [(dirs[(k+1)%len(dirs)] - dirs[k]) % (2*math.pi) for k in range(len(dirs))]
        max_gap = max(gaps)
        spread = 2*math.pi - max_gap
        max_spread = max(max_spread, spread)
    return max_spread

for deg in [10,30,60,90,100,120]:
    rad = math.radians(deg)
    spread = max_angle_spread(rad, n=30)
    print(deg, math.degrees(spread))

[CODE] (turn 2):
import math, random, numpy as np

def compute_sum_M(points, alpha_deg=100):
    alpha = math.radians(alpha_deg)
    total = 0
    n = len(points)
    for i, (xi, yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i==j: continue
            ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi) % (2*math.pi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        # duplicate with +2π for circular sliding
        angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        m = 0
        left = 0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > alpha + 1e-12:
                left += 1
            m = max(m, right-left+1)
        total += m
    return total

def random_cluster(n=100, R=1.0, d=0.9, r=0.05):
    # small disk center at (d,0) inside circle radius R
    points = []
    for _ in range(n):
        # random point in disk radius r
        while True:
            x = random.uniform(-r, r)
            y = random.uniform(-r, r)
            if x*x + y*y <= r*r:
                break
        points.append((d + x, y))
    return points

for r in [0.01, 0.05, 0.1]:
    pts = random_cluster(100, R=1.0, d=0.9, r=r)
    total = compute_sum_M(pts, 100)
    print(r, total)

import math, random, numpy as np

def compute_sum_M(points, alpha_deg=100):
    alpha = math.radians(alpha_deg)
    total = 0
    n = len(points)
    for i, (xi, yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles = []
        for j, (xj, yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i==j: continue
            ang = math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi) % (2*math.pi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        # duplicate with +2π for circular sliding
        angles2 = angles + [a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        m = 0
        left = 0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right] - angles2[left] > alpha + 1e-12:
                left += 1
            m = max(m, right-left+1)
        total += m
    return total

    # small disk center at (d,0) inside circle radius R
    points = []
    for _ in range(n):

            x = random.uniform(-r, r)
            y = random.uniform(-r, r)
            if x*x + y*y <= r*r:
                break
        points.append((d + x, y))
    return points

def line_cluster(n=100, R=1.0, x0=0.9, length=0.2):
    points = []
    for i in range(n):
        y = (i/(n-1)-0.5)*length
        points.append((x0, y))
    return points

[CODE] (turn 8):
def random_points(n=100, R=1.0):
    pts=[]
    for _ in range(n):
        # uniform inside circle radius R
        r=R*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

def avg_random_trials(trials=20):
    totals=[]
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts=random_points(100,1.0)
        totals.append(compute_sum_M(pts,100))
    return sum(totals)/len(totals), max(totals), min(totals)

print(avg_random_trials(10))

def regular_polygon(n=100, R=1.0):
    pts=[]
    for k in range(n):
        theta=2*math.pi*k/n
        pts.append((R*math.cos(theta), R*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

[CODE] (turn 12):
import random, math, numpy as np

def random_point_in_circle(R=1.0):
    r=R*math.sqrt(random.random())
    theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
    return (r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))

def total_sum(points, alpha_deg=100):
    return compute_sum_M(points, alpha_deg)

def hill_climb(n=10, iterations=2000, step=0.05):
    # start with random points
    pts=[random_point_in_circle() for _ in range(n)]
    best=total_sum(pts)
    best_pts=pts[:]
    for it in range(iterations):
        # pick a point to move
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old=pts[i]
        # propose new point near old
        angle=random.random()*2*math.pi
        dr=(random.random()*2-1)*step
        new=(old[0]+dr*math.cos(angle), old[1]+dr*math.sin(angle))
        # ensure inside unit circle
        if new[0]**2+new[1]**2>1.0:
            continue
        pts[i]=new
        cur=total_sum(pts)
        if cur>best:
            best=cur
            best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            # revert with some probability?
            # simple hill climb: revert if not better
            pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts

best10, pts10 = hill_climb(10, iterations=5000, step=0.1)
print("best sum for n=10:", best10)

def compute_Ms(points, alpha_deg=100):
    alpha=math.radians(alpha_deg)
    Ms=[]
    for i,(xi,yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles=[]
        for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i==j: continue
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)%(2*math.pi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        m=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left]>alpha+1e-12:
                left+=1
            m=max(m, right-left+1)
        Ms.append(m)
    return Ms

[CODE] (turn 15):
def optimize(n, iterations=20000, step=0.05):
    pts=[random_point_in_circle() for _ in range(n)]
    best=total_sum(pts)
    best_pts=pts[:]
    for it in range(iterations):
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old=pts[i]
        # propose new point
        angle=random.random()*2*math.pi
        dr=(random.random()*2-1)*step
        new=(old[0]+dr*math.cos(angle), old[1]+dr*math.sin(angle))
        if new[0]**2+new[1]**2>1.0:
            continue
        pts[i]=new
        cur=total_sum(pts)
        if cur>best:
            best=cur
            best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            # accept with probability exp((cur-best)/T) maybe? simple hill climb revert
            pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts

best20, pts20 = optimize(20, iterations=50000, step=0.1)
print(best20)

[CODE] (turn 20):
import random, math

def random_point_in_circle(R=1.0):
    r=R*math.sqrt(random.random())
    theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
    return (r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))

def compute_sum_M(points, alpha_deg=100):
    alpha=math.radians(alpha_deg)
    total=0
    n=len(points)
    for i,(xi,yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles=[]
        for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i==j: continue
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)%(2*math.pi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        m=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left]>alpha+1e-12:
                left+=1
            m=max(m, right-left+1)
        total+=m
    return total

def total_sum(points):
    return compute_sum_M(points,100)

def line_sum(n):
    return sum(max(i, n-1-i) for i in range(n))

def optimize(n, iterations=30000, step=0.1):
    pts=[random_point_in_circle() for _ in range(n)]
    best=total_sum(pts)
    best_pts=pts[:]
    for it in range(iterations):
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old=pts[i]
        angle=random.random()*2*math.pi
        dr=(random.random()*2-1)*step
        new=(old[0]+dr*math.cos(angle), old[1]+dr*math.sin(angle))
        if new[0]**2+new[1]**2>1.0:
            continue
        pts[i]=new
        cur=total_sum(pts)
        if cur>best:
            best=cur
            best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts

print("line sum 20:", line_sum(20))
best20, pts20 = optimize(20, iterations=60000, step=0.12)
print("best found:", best20)

[CODE] (turn 23):
def arc_points(n=100, R=1.0, theta_deg=120):
    theta=math.radians(theta_deg)
    # arc from -theta/2 to +theta/2
    pts=[]
    for k in range(n):
        phi = -theta/2 + theta*k/(n-1)
        pts.append((R*math.cos(phi), R*math.sin(phi)))
    return pts

def sum_for_arc(n, theta_deg):
    pts=arc_points(n,R=1.0,theta_deg=theta_deg)
    return compute_sum_M(pts,100)

for deg in [30,60,90,120,150]:
    print(deg, sum_for_arc(100, deg))

def optimize_n(n=100, iterations=200000, step=0.05):
    pts=[random_point_in_circle() for _ in range(n)]
    best=total_sum(pts)
    best_pts=pts[:]
    for it in range(iterations):
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old=pts[i]
        angle=random.random()*2*math.pi
        dr=(random.random()*2-1)*step
        new=(old[0]+dr*math.cos(angle), old[1]+dr*math.sin(angle))
        if new[0]**2+new[1]**2>1.0:
            continue
        pts[i]=new
        cur=total_sum(pts)
        if cur>best:
            best=cur
            best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts

best100, pts100 = optimize_n(100, iterations=80000, step=0.08)
print(best100)

def small_circle_points(n=100, R_big=1.0, r_small=0.3, c=0.6):
    pts=[]
    for k in range(n):
        theta=2*math.pi*k/n
        x=c + r_small*math.cos(theta)
        y=r_small*math.sin(theta)
        # ensure inside big circle
        if x*x+y*y>R_big*R_big:
            # skip or adjust
            pass
        pts.append((x,y))
    return pts

pts = small_circle_points(100, r_small=0.3, c=0.6)
print(len(pts))
print(compute_sum_M(pts,100))

[CODE] (turn 28):
def thick_line_points(n=100, sigma=0.2):
    pts=[]
    for _ in range(n):
        x=random.uniform(-1,1)
        y=random.gauss(0, sigma)
        # ensure inside unit circle
        if x*x+y*y>1.0:
            # reflect or clamp
            # simple: reject and resample
            while x*x+y*y>1.0:
                x=random.uniform(-1,1)
                y=random.gauss(0, sigma)
        pts.append((x,y))
    return pts

pts=thick_line_points(100, sigma=0.2)
print(compute_sum_M(pts,100))

def thick_line_points(n=100, sigma=0.2):

    for _ in range(n):
        x=random.uniform(-1,1)
        y=random.gauss(0, sigma)
        # ensure inside unit circle
        if x*x+y*y>1.0:
            # reflect or clamp
            # simple: reject and resample
            while x*x+y*y>1.0:
                x=random.uniform(-1,1)
                y=random.gauss(0, sigma)
        pts.append((x,y))
    return pts

[CODE] (turn 29):
import math, random, time

def total_sum(points):
    return compute_sum_M(points,100)

def simulated_annealing(n=100, time_limit=20.0):
    start=time.time()
    pts=[random_point_in_circle() for _ in range(n)]
    best=total_sum(pts)
    best_pts=[p for p in pts]
    cur=best
    T=1.0
    iter=0
    while time.time()-start < time_limit:
        iter+=1
        # gradually cool
        T = max(0.001, 1.0 - (time.time()-start)/time_limit)
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old=pts[i]
        # propose move
        angle=random.random()*2*math.pi
        dr=(random.random()*2-1)*0.1
        new=(old[0]+dr*math.cos(angle), old[1]+dr*math.sin(angle))
        if new[0]**2+new[1]**2>1.0:
            continue
        pts[i]=new
        cur=total_sum(pts)
        delta=cur - best
        if delta>0 or random.random()<math.exp(delta/T):
            if cur>best:
                best=cur
                best_pts=[p for p in pts]
        else:
            pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts, iter

best100, pts100, iters = simulated_annealing(100, time_limit=15.0)
print("best", best100, "iters", iters)

def simulated_annealing(n=100, time_limit=20.0):
    start=time.time()
    pts=[random_point_in_circle() for _ in range(n)]
    best=total_sum(pts)
    best_pts=[p for p in pts]
    cur=best
    T=1.0
    iter=0
    while time.time()-start < time_limit:
        iter+=1
        # gradually cool
        T = max(0.001, 1.0 - (time.time()-start)/time_limit)
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old=pts[i]
        # propose move
        angle=random.random()*2*math.pi
        dr=(random.random()*2-1)*0.1
        new=(old[0]+dr*math.cos(angle), old[1]+dr*math.sin(angle))
        if new[0]**2+new[1]**2>1.0:
            continue
        pts[i]=new
        cur=total_sum(pts)
        delta=cur - best
        if delta>0 or random.random()<math.exp(delta/T):
            if cur>best:
                best=cur
                best_pts=[p for p in pts]
        else:
            pts[i]=old

Ms100=compute_Ms_alpha(pts100)

import math, random, time

def compute_Ms_alpha(points, alpha_deg=100):
    alpha=math.radians(alpha_deg)
    Ms=[]
    n=len(points)
    for i,(xi,yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles=[]
        for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i==j: continue
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)%(2*math.pi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        m=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left]>alpha+1e-12:
                left+=1
            m=max(m, right-left+1)
        Ms.append(m)
    return Ms

# Use the best configuration we previously found (pts100). Need to recompute via SA again quickly.

def random_point_in_circle(R=1.0):
    r=R*math.sqrt(random.random())
    theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
    return (r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))

def total_sum(points):
    return compute_sum_M(points,100)

def simulated_annealing(n=100, time_limit=15.0):
    start=time.time()
    pts=[random_point_in_circle() for _ in range(n)]
    best=total_sum(pts)
    best_pts=[p for p in pts]
    cur=best
    while time.time()-start < time_limit:
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old=pts[i]
        angle=random.random()*2*math.pi
        dr=(random.random()*2-1)*0.1
        new=(old[0]+dr*math.cos(angle), old[1]+dr*math.sin(angle))
        if new[0]**2+new[1]**2>1.0:
            continue
        pts[i]=new
        cur=total_sum(pts)
        if cur>best:
            best=cur
            best_pts=[p for p in pts]
    return best, best_pts

best100, pts100 = simulated_annealing(100, time_limit=12.0)
print("best sum", best100)
Ms100=compute_Ms_alpha(pts100)
print("max", max(Ms100), "min", min(Ms100), "sum", sum(Ms100))

def compute_Ms_alpha(points, alpha_deg=100):

def simulated_annealing_best(n=100, time_limit=25.0):
    start=time.time()
    pts=[random_point_in_circle() for _ in range(n)]
    best=total_sum(pts)
    best_pts=[p for p in pts]
    while time.time()-start < time_limit:
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old=pts[i]
        angle=random.random()*2*math.pi
        dr=(random.random()*2-1)*0.08
        new=(old[0]+dr*math.cos(angle), old[1]+dr*math.sin(angle))
        if new[0]**2+new[1]**2>1.0:

        pts[i]=new
        cur=total_sum(pts)
        if cur>best:
            best=cur
            best_pts=[p for p in pts]
        else:
            # revert with some probability? simple revert
            pts[i]=old
    return best, best_pts

import math, random, itertools, sys, numpy as np

def convex_hull(points):
    # returns points of hull in CCW order, without duplicate last point
    # points: list of (x,y)
    pts = sorted(set(points))
    if len(pts) <= 1:
        return pts
    def cross(o,a,b):
        return (a[0]-o[0])*(b[1]-o[1]) - (a[1]-o[1])*(b[0]-o[0])
    lower=[]
    for p in pts:
        while len(lower)>=2 and cross(lower[-2], lower[-1], p) <= 0:
            lower.pop()
        lower.append(p)
    upper=[]
    for p in reversed(pts):
        while len(upper)>=2 and cross(upper[-2], upper[-1], p) <= 0:
            upper.pop()
        upper.append(p)
    hull = lower[:-1] + upper[:-1]
    return hull

def total_visibility(points, angle_deg=100):
    n=len(points)
    angle_rad=math.radians(angle_deg)
    total=0
    for i,(xi,yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles=[]
        for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i==j: continue
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        # duplicate with +2π
        m=len(angles)
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        # sliding window
        best=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left] > angle_rad+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            if cnt>best:
                best=cnt
        total+=best
    return total

def random_points_in_circle(n, radius=1.0):
    pts=[]
    for _ in range(n):
        r=radius*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

def test_random(n, trials=200):
    best=0
    best_pts=None
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts=random_points_in_circle(n)
        val=total_visibility(pts)
        if val>best:
            best=val
            best_pts=pts
    return best, best_pts

for n in range(3,11):
    best,_=test_random(n, trials=2000)
    print(n, best)

import math, random, itertools, sys, numpy as np

def convex_hull(points):
    # returns points of hull in CCW order, without duplicate last point
    # points: list of (x,y)
    pts = sorted(set(points))
    if len(pts) <= 1:
        return pts
    def cross(o,a,b):
        return (a[0]-o[0])*(b[1]-o[1]) - (a[1]-o[1])*(b[0]-o[0])
    lower=[]
    for p in pts:
        while len(lower)>=2 and cross(lower[-2], lower[-1], p) <= 0:
            lower.pop()
        lower.append(p)
    upper=[]
    for p in reversed(pts):
        while len(upper)>=2 and cross(upper[-2], upper[-1], p) <= 0:
            upper.pop()
        upper.append(p)
    hull = lower[:-1] + upper[:-1]
    return hull

def total_visibility(points, angle_deg=100):
    n=len(points)
    angle_rad=math.radians(angle_deg)
    total=0
    for i,(xi,yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles=[]
        for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i==j: continue
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        # duplicate with +2π
        m=len(angles)
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        # sliding window
        best=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left] > angle_rad+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            if cnt>best:
                best=cnt
        total+=best
    return total

def random_points_in_circle(n, radius=1.0):
    pts=[]
    for _ in range(n):
        r=radius*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

    best=0
    best_pts=None
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts=random_points_in_circle(n)
        val=total_visibility(pts)
        if val>best:
            best=val
            best_pts=pts
    return best, best_pts

def points_on_arc(n, R=1.0, start_angle=0.0, arc_angle=math.radians(30)):
    # equally spaced points along arc
    pts=[]
    for k in range(n):
        theta = start_angle + arc_angle * k/(n-1) if n>1 else start_angle
        pts.append((R*math.cos(theta), R*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

def total_visibility_points(pts):
    return total_visibility(pts, angle_deg=100)

for arc_deg in [10,20,30,40,50,60,80,100]:
    pts=points_on_arc(100, R=1.0, start_angle=0.0, arc_angle=math.radians(arc_deg))
    val=total_visibility_points(pts)
    print(arc_deg, val)

[CODE] (turn 8):
import random, math, copy, time

def total_visibility_fast(points, angle_deg=100):
    # O(n^2 log n) as before
    return total_visibility(points, angle_deg)

def hill_climb(n=100, iterations=20000, radius=1.0):
    # start with points on circle equally spaced
    pts=[(radius*math.cos(2*math.pi*i/n), radius*math.sin(2*math.pi*i/n)) for i in range(n)]
    best_val=total_visibility_fast(pts)
    best_pts=pts[:]
    val=best_val
    for it in range(iterations):
        # pick random point to move
        i=random.randrange(n)
        # propose new location within circle radius
        r=radius*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
        new_pt=(r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))
        old_pt=pts[i]
        pts[i]=new_pt
        new_val=total_visibility_fast(pts)
        if new_val>=val:
            val=new_val
            if new_val>best_val:
                best_val=new_val
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            # revert
            pts[i]=old_pt
    return best_val, best_pts

best_val, best_pts = hill_climb(iterations=2000)
best_val

[CODE] (turn 10):
def hill_climb_iter(n=100, iterations=5000, radius=1.0):
    pts=[(radius*math.cos(2*math.pi*i/n), radius*math.sin(2*math.pi*i/n)) for i in range(n)]
    val=total_visibility_fast(pts)
    best_val=val
    best_pts=pts[:]
    for it in range(iterations):
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old_pt=pts[i]
        # propose new location near old point (small move)
        # random displacement within radius*0.2
        dr=radius*0.2* (random.random()*2-1)
        dtheta=2*math.pi*random.random()
        # Actually move in polar coordinates relative to origin
        r=math.hypot(old_pt[0], old_pt[1])
        theta=math.atan2(old_pt[1], old_pt[0])
        # add small random offset
        r_new = max(0, min(radius, r + dr))
        theta_new = theta + (random.random()*0.2-0.1) # small angle change
        new_pt=(r_new*math.cos(theta_new), r_new*math.sin(theta_new))
        pts[i]=new_pt
        new_val=total_visibility_fast(pts)
        if new_val>=val:
            val=new_val
            if new_val>best_val:
                best_val=new_val
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old_pt
    return best_val, best_pts

best_val, best_pts = hill_climb_iter(iterations=5000)
best_val

[CODE] (turn 10):
def hill_climb_iter(n=100, iterations=5000, radius=1.0):
    pts=[(radius*math.cos(2*math.pi*i/n), radius*math.sin(2*math.pi*i/n)) for i in range(n)]
    val=total_visibility_fast(pts)
    best_val=val
    best_pts=pts[:]
    for it in range(iterations):
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old_pt=pts[i]
        # propose new location near old point (small move)
        # random displacement within radius*0.2
        dr=radius*0.2* (random.random()*2-1)
        dtheta=2*math.pi*random.random()
        # Actually move in polar coordinates relative to origin
        r=math.hypot(old_pt[0], old_pt[1])
        theta=math.atan2(old_pt[1], old_pt[0])
        # add small random offset
        r_new = max(0, min(radius, r + dr))
        theta_new = theta + (random.random()*0.2-0.1) # small angle change
        new_pt=(r_new*math.cos(theta_new), r_new*math.sin(theta_new))
        pts[i]=new_pt
        new_val=total_visibility_fast(pts)
        if new_val>=val:
            val=new_val
            if new_val>best_val:
                best_val=new_val
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old_pt
    return best_val, best_pts

best_val, best_pts = hill_climb_iter(iterations=5000)
best_val

[CODE] (turn 11):
def total_visibility(points, angle_deg=100):
    n=len(points)
    angle_rad=math.radians(angle_deg)
    total=0
    for i,(xi,yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles=[]
        for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i==j: continue
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        best=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left] > angle_rad+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            if cnt>best:
                best=cnt
        total+=best
    return total

def total_visibility_fast(points):
    return total_visibility(points, angle_deg=100)

[CODE] (turn 13):
def hill_climb_global(n=100, iterations=5000, radius=1.0):
    pts=[(radius*math.cos(2*math.pi*i/n), radius*math.sin(2*math.pi*i/n)) for i in range(n)]
    val=total_visibility_fast(pts)
    best_val=val
    best_pts=pts[:]
    for it in range(iterations):
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old_pt=pts[i]
        # propose new random point inside circle
        r=radius*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
        new_pt=(r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))
        pts[i]=new_pt
        new_val=total_visibility_fast(pts)
        if new_val>=val:
            val=new_val
            if new_val>best_val:
                best_val=new_val
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old_pt
    return best_val, best_pts

best_val, best_pts = hill_climb_global(iterations=5000)
best_val

import random, math

def total_visibility(points, angle_deg=100):
    n=len(points)
    angle_rad=math.radians(angle_deg)
    total=0
    for i,(xi,yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles=[]
        for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i==j: continue
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        best=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left] > angle_rad+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            if cnt>best:
                best=cnt
        total+=best
    return total

def hill_climb_global(n=100, iterations=5000, radius=1.0):
    pts=[(radius*math.cos(2*math.pi*i/n), radius*math.sin(2*math.pi*i/n)) for i in range(n)]
    val=total_visibility(pts)
    best_val=val
    best_pts=pts[:]
    for it in range(iterations):
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old_pt=pts[i]
        r=radius*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
        new_pt=(r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))
        pts[i]=new_pt
        new_val=total_visibility(pts)
        if new_val>=val:
            val=new_val
            if new_val>best_val:
                best_val=new_val
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old_pt
    return best_val, best_pts

[CODE] (turn 17):
def improve(initial_pts, iterations, radius=1.0):
    pts=initial_pts[:]
    val=total_visibility(pts)
    best_val=val
    best_pts=pts[:]
    n=len(pts)
    for it in range(iterations):
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old_pt=pts[i]
        r=radius*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
        new_pt=(r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))
        pts[i]=new_pt
        new_val=total_visibility(pts)
        if new_val>=val:
            val=new_val
            if new_val>best_val:
                best_val=new_val
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old_pt
    return best_val, best_pts, val, pts

best_val, best_pts = hill_climb_global(iterations=6000)
# continue improvement
for batch in range(5):
    best_val, best_pts, cur_val, cur_pts = improve(best_pts, iterations=4000)
    print("batch", batch, "best", best_val)

def improve(initial_pts, iterations, radius=1.0):
    pts=initial_pts[:]
    val=total_visibility(pts)
    best_val=val
    best_pts=pts[:]
    n=len(pts)
    for it in range(iterations):
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old_pt=pts[i]
        r=radius*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
        new_pt=(r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))

        new_val=total_visibility(pts)
        if new_val>=val:
            val=new_val
            if new_val>best_val:
                best_val=new_val
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old_pt
    return best_val, best_pts, val, pts

# continue improvement

def best_random(n, trials=2000):
    best=0
    for _ in range(trials):
        pts=random_points_in_circle(n, radius=1.0)
        val=total_visibility(pts)
        if val>best:

    return best

[CODE] (turn 19):
def random_points_in_circle(n, radius=1.0):
    pts=[]
    for _ in range(n):
        r=radius*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
        pts.append((r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta)))
    return pts

for n in range(3,11):
    best=best_random(n, trials=2000)
    print(n, best, best/(n*(n-1)))

def compute_ai(points):
    n=len(points)
    angle_rad=math.radians(100)
    a=[]
    for i,(xi,yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles=[]
        for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i==j: continue
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        best=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left] > angle_rad+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            if cnt>best:
                best=cnt
        a.append(best)
    return a

[CODE] (turn 24):
def compute_ai(points):
    n=len(points)
    angle_rad=math.radians(100)
    a=[]
    for i,(xi,yi) in enumerate(points):
        angles=[]
        for j,(xj,yj) in enumerate(points):
            if i==j: continue
            ang=math.atan2(yj-yi, xj-xi)
            angles.append(ang)
        angles.sort()
        angles2=angles+[a+2*math.pi for a in angles]
        best=0
        left=0
        for right in range(len(angles2)):
            while angles2[right]-angles2[left] > angle_rad+1e-12:
                left+=1
            cnt=right-left+1
            if cnt>best:
                best=cnt
        a.append(best)
    return a

ai10=compute_ai(pts10)
ai10, sum(ai10)

[CODE] (turn 25):
def hull_indices(points):
    # returns indices of hull points in order
    pts = [(x,y,i) for i,(x,y) in enumerate(points)]
    # sort by x then y
    pts_sorted = sorted(pts, key=lambda p:(p[0],p[1]))
    def cross(o,a,b):
        return (a[0]-o[0])*(b[1]-o[1]) - (a[1]-o[1])*(b[0]-o[0])
    lower=[]
    for p in pts_sorted:
        while len(lower)>=2 and cross(lower[-2], lower[-1], p) <= 0:
            lower.pop()
        lower.append(p)
    upper=[]
    for p in reversed(pts_sorted):
        while len(upper)>=2 and cross(upper[-2], upper[-1], p) <= 0:
            upper.pop()
        upper.append(p)
    hull = lower[:-1] + upper[:-1]
    return [p[2] for p in hull]

hull_idx = hull_indices(best_pts)
len(hull_idx)

[CODE] (turn 27):
def hill_climb_global(n=100, iterations=2000, radius=1.0):
    pts=[(radius*math.cos(2*math.pi*i/n), radius*math.sin(2*math.pi*i/n)) for i in range(n)]
    val=total_visibility(pts)
    best_val=val
    best_pts=pts[:]
    for it in range(iterations):
        i=random.randrange(n)
        old_pt=pts[i]
        r=radius*math.sqrt(random.random())
        theta=random.random()*2*math.pi
        new_pt=(r*math.cos(theta), r*math.sin(theta))
        pts[i]=new_pt
        new_val=total_visibility(pts)
        if new_val>=val:
            val=new_val
            if new_val>best_val:
                best_val=new_val
                best_pts=pts[:]
        else:
            pts[i]=old_pt
    return best_val, best_pts

best_val, best_pts = hill_climb_global(iterations=5000)
best_val
